12. Evaluation and Experimentation: Offline Metrics, Off-Policy Evaluation, A/B Platforms, Interleaving and Long-Term Measurement

Overview

   COST / LATENCY OF A DECISION                         FIDELITY TO BUSINESS GOAL
   low                                                                       high
   +-----------+   +-------------+   +--------------+   +---------+   +-----------+
   | Offline   |-->| Off-policy  |-->| Interleaving |-->|  A/B    |-->| Long-term |
   | metrics   |   | evaluation  |   | (ranking)    |   |  test   |   | holdout / |
   | NDCG,AUC  |   | IPS, DR     |   | team-draft   |   | 1-4 wks |   | surrogate |
   +-----------+   +-------------+   +--------------+   +---------+   +-----------+
   minutes-hours     hours             days               weeks          months
   1000s of ideas    100s              10s                handful        portfolio

12.1.3 Pitfalls of offline evaluation

  OFFLINE EVAL PIPELINE (typical)
  impression+engagement logs ---> time-based split ----> train window | eval window
                                                                        |
     candidate model --(score full catalogue or logged slate)-----------+
                                                                        v
           +----------------+--------------+---------------+------------------+
           | retrieval:     | ranking:     | calibration:  | beyond-accuracy: |
           | recall@K       | NDCG, AUC,   | sum(p)/sum(y) | coverage, KL     |
           | (full catalog) | NE/logloss   | by slice      | genre calib.,    |
           |                | per slice    | reliability   | diversity, nov.  |
           +----------------+--------------+---------------+------------------+
                     \__________________ gate: "worth an online test?" ______/

12.2.5 Tooling and benchmarks

  OPE DATA FLOW
   request --> logging policy pi_0 (ranker + sampler) --> slate shown
                         |  logs: x, slate, per-slot p(a|x), model_version
                         v
                 [ propensity-aware log store ]
                         |
     candidate pi ------>+-----> IPS / SNIPS / DR / PI estimators
     reward model r_hat->+            |
                                      +--> V_hat(pi), CI, ESS
                                      v
                  gate: V_hat significantly > V_hat(pi_0) and ESS ok
                         --> interleaving / A/B

12.3.1 Method

  TEAM-DRAFT INTERLEAVING
  Ranker A: a1 a2 a3 a4 ...           Ranker B: b1 a1 b2 b3 ...
  round 1: coin=A -> A picks a1 ; B picks b1
  round 2: coin=B -> B picks b2 (a1 taken) ; A picks a2
  ...
  merged list: [a1(A), b1(B), b2(B), a2(A), ...]
  user engages with b1, b2  --> preference(B) for this user
  aggregate over users: Delta = (#users prefer A - #users prefer B) / N  -> sign test

12.4 A/B testing platforms

  CANONICAL EXPERIMENTATION PLATFORM
  +-------------------+     +--------------------------+     +---------------------+
  | Experiment config |---->| Assignment / allocation  |---->| Product services    |
  | UI + API (owners, |     | hash(unit_id, salt)%N -> |     | read variant, emit  |
  | metrics, layers,  |     | bucket -> variant; layers|     | exposure events     |
  | targeting, ramps) |     | / domains for overlap    |     +----------+----------+
  +-------------------+     +--------------------------+                |
                                                                        v
  +-------------------+     +--------------------------+     +---------------------+
  | Scorecards / UI   |<----| Stats engine: deltas,    |<----| Metrics pipeline    |
  | alerts, guardrail |     | CIs, CUPED/variance red, |     | (batch DAGs: exposure|
  | SRM checks, ship  |     | sequential tests, SRM,   |     | x metric joins,      |
  | decision records  |     | multiple-test control    |     | per-unit aggregates) |
  +-------------------+     +--------------------------+     +---------------------+

12.5.2 Surrogate index

  SURROGATE-INDEX WORKFLOW
  historical cohorts: short-term S (wk1-4 engagement) --> long-term Y (retention 6-12 mo)
                     \____ fit f(S) ~ E[Y|S]  (validated on past A/Bs) ____/
  new A/B (2-4 wks):   S_treat, S_ctrl  --> f(S_treat) - f(S_ctrl) = estimated effect on Y
  safety net:          long-term holdout periodically checks that f still predicts Y

12.6 Putting it together: a reference evaluation architecture

                         RECOMMENDER EVALUATION REFERENCE ARCHITECTURE
  +--------------------------- LOGGING (serve time) -------------------------------+
  | request_id, unit_id, experiment assignments, model_version, candidate set,      |
  | final slate, per-slot propensity p(a|x), features snapshot, exposure event      |
  +----------+-------------------------------------+--------------------------------+
             |                                     |
             v                                     v
  +---------------------+                 +--------------------------+
  | OFFLINE EVAL        |                 | OPE SERVICE              |
  | full-catalogue      |                 | IPS/SNIPS/DR/PI on       |
  | recall@K, NDCG, NE, |                 | exploration logs; ESS;   |
  | calibration slices, |                 | CI; compares vs pi_0     |
  | beyond-accuracy     |                 +------------+-------------+
  +----------+----------+                              |
             +-------------------+---------------------+
                                 v
                    +--------------------------+   triage 10s of candidates
                    | INTERLEAVING (ranking)   |   ~1-2 orders of mag. more sensitive
                    +------------+-------------+
                                 v
                    +--------------------------+   OEC + guardrails + SRM + CUPED
                    | A/B PLATFORM             |   sequential monitoring
                    +------------+-------------+
                                 v
                    +--------------------------+   surrogate index predicts LT
                    | LONG-TERM LAYER          |   holdouts validate cumulative
                    +--------------------------+   impact & surrogate models

13. Exploration, Bandits and Reinforcement Learning in Recommendation

1. The problem: closed loops and missing counterfactuals

   +---------+   context x    +------------+  action a, p(a|x)   +---------+
   | request |--------------->|  policy pi |-------------------->|  user   |
   +---------+                | (explore)  |                     +----+----+
                              +-----+------+                          |
                                    ^                          reward r (delayed)
                                    | deploy                          |
             +-----------------+    |    +---------------------------v------+
             |  off-policy     |----+----|   log: (x, a, p(a|x), r, t)      |
             |  eval (IPS/DR)  |<--------|   joined on impression id        |
             +-----------------+         +----------------------------------+
                     ^                                    |
                     +------------- train pi' ------------+

Design pattern

  Bayesian CTR model (per-feature mean mu_j, variance s_j^2)
          |
          |  at request time
          v
  sample w_j ~ N(mu_j, s_j^2)  ---->  score = sigmoid(sum_j w_j x_j)  -> rank
          |
          |  after feedback (batched)
          v
  posterior update (ADF / Laplace)  --> shrink variance on features seen often

Logging and offline evaluation

   page build request
         |
         v
   +--------------------+   for each title on the page
   | artwork policy     |------------------------------+
   | (contextual model  |                              |
   |  + exploration)    |   image id, propensity p      |
   +---------+----------+                              v
             |                              +---------------------+
             |  render                      | impression log      |
             v                              | (member, title,     |
     member sees image ---- play? -------->| image, p, context)  |
                                            +----------+----------+
                                                       |
                         +-----------------------------v-------------+
                         | offline replay: keep events where the new  |
                         | policy picks the logged image; compute take |
                         | fraction (plays / impressions)             |
                         +-------------------------------------------+

BaRT: Bandits for Recommendations as Treatments

   candidates (items) x explanations  --->  FM reward model r_hat(u, i, e)
                                                     |
                                                     v
                                  epsilon-greedy over (i, e) per shelf slot
                                                     |
                     log (u, i, e, p, context) ------+---> home page shelf
                                                     |
                     stream >= threshold seconds? ---+---> reward
                                                     |
                     propensity-weighted retraining <+

Architecture

   user event sequence (watched videos, context)
               |
               v
      +-----------------+
      |   RNN state s_t |  (shared user-state encoder)
      +--------+--------+
               |
       +-------+---------------------------+
       |                                   |
       v                                   v
  softmax pi_theta(a|s)            softmax beta_hat(a|s)
  over millions of videos          (behavior policy head;
  (the policy we deploy)            gradient blocked into
       |                            the shared encoder)
       |                                   |
       +------> importance weight w = pi_theta / beta_hat
                       |
                       v
       top-K correction lambda_K = K * (1 - pi_theta)^(K-1)
                       |
                       v
       REINFORCE gradient: sum_t  w * lambda_K * R_t * grad log pi_theta(a_t|s_t)
       (with weight capping and normalized importance sampling)

Slate optimization

   offline logs (s, slate A, clicked i, r, s')
            |
            +--> choice model P(i | s, A)   (trained as a classifier)
            |
            +--> Q_bar(s, i) via SARSA/TD on (s, i, r, s', i')
                          |
   serving:  candidates from retrieval (hundreds)
                          |
            score each i by P-weighted Q_bar
                          |
            slate = LP or greedy top-k  ---> user

Kuaishou: reinforcing user retention at billion scale (WWW 2023)

  request t:  user state s_t (history, profile, time since last visit)
                 |
                 v
          actor pi(s_t) --> weights w_t = (w_watch, w_like, w_follow, ...)
                 |
                 v
     existing ranker: score(i) = sum_k w_t,k * prediction_k(i)   ---> feed
                 |
   session ends; next visit after gap g  ---> reward r = f(g) (+ shaped terms)
                 |
          critic V(s) trained by TD; actor updated by critic

Reference architecture

                     +-------------------------------------------+
                     |               SERVING PATH                |
  request ---------> | retrieval -> ranking (predictions p_k)    |
                     |     |             |                       |
                     |     |    value / policy layer:            |
                     |     |    - bandit / TS sampling           |
                     |     |    - RL fusion weights w(s)         |
                     |     |    - slate optimizer (LP / greedy)  |
                     |     v             v                       |
                     |   final slate + propensities p(a|x)       |
                     +-------------------+-----------------------+
                                         |
                                         v
        +-----------------------------------------------------------+
        |  impression log (request id, context, slate, positions,   |
        |  propensities, model versions, exploration flag)          |
        +-----------------------------+-----------------------------+
                                      |  join rewards (immediate +
                                      |  delayed, with windows)
                                      v
        +-----------------------------------------------------------+
        |  training: IPS / SNIPS-weighted losses, TD critics,       |
        |  behavior-policy estimation, reward models                |
        +-----------------------------+-----------------------------+
                                      v
        +-----------------------------------------------------------+
        |  OPE gate: DR / SNIPS / replay on exploration bucket,     |
        |  effective-sample-size checks, simulator smoke tests      |
        +-----------------------------+-----------------------------+
                                      v
                       online A/B test -> rollout

14. Debiasing, Diversity, Fairness and Cold Start: The Corrective Layers of Production Recommenders

Overview

                 +-------------------- feedback loop --------------------+
                 |                                                        |
 logs (biased) --+--> [A] debiasing in training -----> retrieval / ranking |
                      - position towers (YouTube, Airbnb, Huawei PAL)      |
                      - IPS / propensity weighting (Joachims, Schnabel)    |
                      - logQ sampling correction (YouTube two-tower)       |
                      - popularity deconfounding (PDA, MACR)               |
                      - duration deconfounding (Kuaishou D2Q)              |
                                                     |                     |
                                                     v                     |
                      [B] slate construction / re-ranking                  |
                      - MMR, DPP (YouTube, Hulu), calibration (Netflix)    |
                      - listwise diversity (Airbnb)                        |
                      - fairness constraints (LinkedIn, Spotify)           |
                                                     |                     |
                                                     v                     |
                      [C] cold start and exploration                       |
                      - content towers, meta-embeddings, DropoutNet        |
                      - cohort gating (Kuaishou POSO), bandits (Yahoo)     |
                      - new-listing priors (Airbnb)                        |
                                                     |                     |
                                                     v                     |
                                            impressions shown to user -----+

Architecture.

 user / context / candidate features          position, device (+ other bias feats)
                 |                                        |
         +---------------+                         +--------------+
         | shared hidden |                         | shallow tower|
         |  layers       |                         | (small MLP / |
         +---------------+                         |  linear)     |
                 |                                 +--------------+
         +---------------+                                |
         | MMoE experts  |                                |
         | + task gates  |                                |
         +---------------+                                |
           |         |                                    |
   engagement     satisfaction                            |
   task towers    task towers                             |
           |                                              |
       logit_main  ------------------ (+) --------------- logit_bias
                                       |
                                    sigmoid ----> training loss (click etc.)

  Serving: the position feature is treated as missing, so logit_bias collapses
           to a constant and ranking uses the main towers only.

14.3.5 Duration bias in watch time: Kuaishou D2Q

      duration --------> exposure (system shows long videos more)
         |                  |
         v                  v
               watch time (label)

  D2Q: split training data into duration buckets
       -> within each bucket, convert watch time into a quantile label
       -> fit one model (shared parameters) predicting the within-bucket quantile
       -> serve on quantiles: compares "how well this video holds attention
          relative to similar-length videos"

Architecture.

 candidate generators --> pointwise ranker (q_i = predicted utility)
                                   |
                                   v  top-N ranked candidates (N ~ a few hundred, approx.)
                    +-------------------------------+
                    | DPP re-ranker                 |
                    |  kernel:                      |
                    |  L_ii = q_i^2                 |
                    |  L_ij = alpha*q_i*q_j*        |
                    |        exp(-D_ij / (2 s^2))   |
                    |  D_ij from video embeddings / |
                    |  topical distance             |
                    |  greedy MAP, window by window |
                    +-------------------------------+
                                   |
                                   v
                   feed served in windows of k items; next window
                   chosen conditioned on items already selected

14.6 Cold Start

             +-------------------+      +------------------------+
 new item -->| content encoders  |----> | ID embedding warm-up   |--> ranker
 (meta,     | (text, image,     |      | (meta-embedding [25],   |
  media)    |  audio, creator)  |      |  DropoutNet [24])       |
             +-------------------+      +------------------------+
                      |                             ^
                      v                             |
            exploration traffic (bandits [32], boosted slots, priors [5])
                      |
                      v
            real-time training (minutes) -> ID embedding converges

 new user --> onboarding signals + context (geo, device, referrer)
          --> cohort-specific gating (POSO [26]) --> popular-within-cohort fallback

14.7.2 LinkedIn Recruiter: fairness-aware re-ranking at scale

 query --> candidate retrieval --> ML ranker (relevance scores)
                                         |
                                         v
            desired distribution p_q(attribute) = distribution among candidates
            matching the query (e.g., qualified pool), computed per query
                                         |
                                         v
            fairness re-ranker: per-attribute queues sorted by score;
            fill ranks greedily so that every prefix k has at least
            floor(p_a * k) and at most ceil(p_a * k) of each attribute a
            (DetGreedy / DetCons / DetRelaxed variants)
                                         |
                                         v
                              top-k shown to recruiter

14.8 Putting It Together: A Reference Corrective Stack

 [Training data]
   impressions + engagements + position + device + example age + duration
     |
     |-- reweight: fixed examples per user [12]; IPS for explicit ratings [13]
     v
 [Retrieval: two-tower]
   in-batch softmax with logQ correction via streaming frequency estimator [11]
   ID dropout so content towers handle cold items [24]
   meta-embedding initialisation for new IDs [25]
     |
     v  ~ thousands of candidates
 [Ranker: multitask DNN / MMoE]
   main towers (engagement, satisfaction)
   + shallow position/device tower, additive logit, position dropout [1][5]
   duration-bucketed quantile targets for watch time [27]
   cohort gates for new users [26]
   example_age = 0 and position = missing at serving [12][1]
     |
     v  ~ hundreds of candidates
 [Re-ranking / slate construction]
   fast greedy DPP or MMR within sliding windows [2][3][10]
   calibration to user's genre / creator-popularity mix [4]
   fairness prefix constraints where policy requires [21]
   exploration slots for cold items/creators (bandit bonus) [32]
     |
     v
 [Serving + logging]  log true positions, exploration flags, propensities
                      (needed by every debiasing method above)

15. Re-ranking, Blending and Whole-Page Optimization

1.1 Canonical last-mile architecture

 heavy ranker output: ~10^1-10^3 candidates, each with p_click, p_like, p_hide, ...
        |
        v
 +--------------------+   value model: score = sum w_i * p_i (+ offsets)
 | 1. score fusion    |   (Ch. 4: Meta value model, X weights, Kuaishou ensemble sort)
 +---------+----------+
           v
 +--------------------+   hard filters: integrity, blocked/muted, seen, legal,
 | 2. eligibility     |   inventory, geo, age, dedup by item / cluster
 +---------+----------+
           v
 +--------------------+   context-aware rescoring: DLCM / PRM / SetRank / PEAR
 | 3. listwise rerank |   or generator + evaluator (Seq2Slate, GRN, PIER, NAR4Rec)
 +---------+----------+
           v
 +--------------------+   MMR / DPP / author decay / calibration / cold-start lift
 | 4. diversification |
 +---------+----------+
           v
 +--------------------+   interleave content types and sources, insert ads at
 | 5. blending / slots|   allowed positions, modules (WTF, stories, carousels)
 +---------+----------+
           v
 +--------------------+   rows / shelves / modules chosen and ordered;
 | 6. page assembly   |   cross-row dedup; device templates
 +---------+----------+
           v
     response + impression log (slate, positions, propensities)

2.3 Multiplicative decays and lifts (X / Twitter)

  weighted sum of ~25 heads  -> negative-score offset (keeps scores > 0)
     -> + exploration bonus
     -> x author-diversity multiplier: (1 - floor) * decay^k + floor
            (k = number of higher-ranked posts already chosen from that author)
     -> x out-of-network factor (< 1 for unfollowed accounts)
     -> cold-start lift (new author raised to at least a target rank)
     -> DPP reorder (vm-ranker) -> visibility filters -> timeline

Architecture.

  initial list from ranker: items i_1 .. i_n  (n = list length, tens of items)
        |
        v
  per item input  x_i = [ item feature vector  ||  PV_i ]  +  PE_i
        |               PV_i = personalized vector: hidden layer of a pre-trained
        |                      CTR model taking (user, item) -> user-specific item rep
        |               PE_i = learnable position embedding (initial rank)
        v
  linear projection to d_model
        v
  N x Transformer encoder blocks (multi-head self-attention + FFN)
        |   every item attends to every other item -> O(n^2) but n is small
        v
  linear + softmax over the list  -> re-ranked scores
        v
  sort -> final list

4.2 Generator-evaluator: Exact-K, PRS, GRN

            candidates (N)                      evaluator (list-wise CTR / utility model)
                |                                       ^
                v                                       | score each candidate permutation
   +---------------------------+     M candidate    +---+----------------------------+
   | generator: beam search /  | --> permutations-->| list-aware model: sees full   |
   | sampling / pointer net /  |     (M ~ tens-     | slate incl. later positions;  |
   | heuristic permutations    |      hundreds)     | predicts list reward          |
   +---------------------------+                    +---+----------------------------+
                                                        v
                                         pick argmax permutation -> serve

6.1 LinkedIn: first-pass rankers and a second-pass ranker

   FollowFeed FPR ----+   (network updates; >80% of feed items in 2019 [22])
   Jobs FPR ----------+
   Recommended FPR ---+--> SPR: unified feature space, multi-objective score
   PYMK / modules ----+        (e.g. viral actions, contributions, creator side)
   Ads (separate auction) ------------+
                                      v
                          slotting: ads at allowed positions -> feed

6.5 Ads and organic: the allocation problem

 organic ranked list O = [o1, o2, ...]       ad list A = [a1, a2, ...] (auctioned)
                \                                   /
                 v                                 v
          +---------------------------------------------------+
          | allocator                                         |
          |  - candidate slots: fixed (e.g. every k-th) or    |
          |    dynamic                                        |
          |  - constraints: min gap between ads, max ad load  |
          |    per session, no ad in slot 1, category rules   |
          |  - objective: revenue(a, pos) + lambda *          |
          |    engagement_loss(pos)                           |
          |  - solver: greedy / DP over slots / RL policy     |
          +---------------------------------------------------+
                                  v
                       final feed + ad-load telemetry

7.1 Netflix: page generation

  candidate rows: genre rows (PVR), Top Picks, Because-You-Watched, Trending,
                  Continue Watching, New Releases, ...  (each row ranks its own titles)
        |
        v
  row scoring  (relevance of row to member, given context/device)
        |
        v
  stage-wise greedy page construction:
     for slot in 1..R:
        pick row maximizing  score(row | rows already placed)
          - penalize duplicate titles across rows (dedup / diversity)
          - enforce constraints (e.g. Continue Watching near top, device limits,
            min/max of row types)
        |
        v
  render: rows top-to-bottom, titles left-to-right (2-D attention)

8.2 Design patterns

 config store (versioned, A/B-able)
      |  rules as data: {id, scope(surface, country), type(filter|cap|boost|slot),
      |                  params, priority, experiment_id}
      v
 +-------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | rule engine in the mixer                                                |
 |  1. hard filters (set operations; cheap; run early AND re-run at end)   |
 |  2. score adjustments (multipliers/offsets; order-preserving sign care) |
 |  3. sequential constraints (greedy fill: author cap, gap rules, DPP)    |
 |  4. slot constraints (ads, modules, pinned rows)                        |
 |  5. backfill if constraints leave holes (fallback sources)              |
 +-------------------------------------------------------------------------+
      |
      v  log: which rule touched which item (for debugging and OPE)

16. Ads Recommendation Systems: pCTR/pCVR Prediction, Auctions, Pacing, Calibration and the Meta, Google, Pinterest, Snap and Twitter Ads Stacks

Overview

 ad request (user, context, placement)
      |
      v
 +-----------------+   eligibility: targeting, budget left, policy, frequency caps
 | ad index / ACL  |   10^6 .. 10^7+ eligible ads (Meta: "tens of millions") [10][11]
 +--------+--------+
          v
 +-----------------+   two-tower / learned index (Andromeda) / GPU brute force
 | retrieval       |   -> 10^3 .. 10^4 candidates
 +--------+--------+
          v
 +-----------------+   cheap model, often distilled from the heavy ranker
 | light ranking   |   -> 10^2 candidates
 +--------+--------+
          v
 +-----------------+   multi-task DNN: pCTR, pCVR (per conversion type), quality,
 | heavy ranking   |   negative feedback; calibrated per task
 +--------+--------+
          v
 +-----------------+   score = bid(or auto-bid) x p(action) + quality terms
 | auction         |   allocation + pricing (GSP-like, VCG-like), reserve prices
 +--------+--------+
          v
 +-----------------+   ad load, spacing, slot choice vs organic items
 | allocation      |
 +--------+--------+
          v
 impression --> click/conversion logs --> joiner --> online trainer (minutes-hours)
          ^                                                  |
          +-------------- pacing / bid controller <----------+ (spend telemetry)

3.1 Practical Lessons from Predicting Clicks on Ads at Facebook (ADKDD 2014)

 raw features --> boosted trees T1..TK (retrained ~daily)
                    | leaf ids (one-hot)
                    v
              logistic regression (online SGD, near real time)
                    |
                    v
          p (downsampled space) --> q = p / (p + (1 - p) / w) --> auction

4.3 Sample-selection bias: ESMM

 impression features --> shared embeddings
                              |                 |
                          CTR tower         CVR tower
                              |                 |
                            pCTR ------(x)---- pCVR
                              |                 |
                     loss on click       pCTCVR: loss on click & convert

7.1 GSP, VCG and why ads use "GSP-like" auctions

 rank by  score_i = bid_i x pCTR_i (x pCVR_i) + quality_i         (expected value)
 slot k goes to the k-th highest score
 GSP price per click for winner k:
     cpc_k = (score_{k+1} - quality_k) / pCTR_k     (+ minimum increment, >= reserve)
 => winner k pays just enough to beat k+1, so pCTR errors move both rank AND price

throttling (participate in a fraction of eligible auctions) and bid modification

 advertiser goal (budget B, target CPA)          spend telemetry (per minute)
           |                                               |
           v                                               v
 +-------------------+   multiplier lambda_t   +--------------------------+
 | bid controller /  |------------------------>| auction: bid_t = lambda_t |
 | RL policy         |<------------------------|  x pCVR x target CPA      |
 +-------------------+  spend, conversions,    +--------------------------+
     state: remaining budget, time of day, pace error

9. Ad load and allocation against organic content

 organic ranked list O = [o1, o2, ...]      auctioned ad list A = [a1, a2, ...]
                \                                 /
                 v                               v
        +----------------------------------------------------+
        | allocator                                          |
        |  slots: fixed (every k-th) or dynamic per user     |
        |  constraints: min gap, max ad load per session,    |
        |               no ad in slot 1, category rules      |
        |  objective: revenue(a, pos) + lambda x             |
        |             engagement_loss(pos)                   |
        +----------------------------------------------------+

17. Google & YouTube Case Studies: From Co-Visitation Graphs to Semantic IDs and LLM-Native Recommenders

Overview

 Google / YouTube recommender lineage (simplified)

 2007 Google News  --- MinHash + PLSI + co-visitation (MapReduce/Bigtable)
 2010 YouTube      --- co-visitation related graph, seed expansion, heuristic rank
 2016 Google Play  --- Wide & Deep (memorization + generalization)
 2016 YouTube      --- DNN candidate gen (softmax -> ANN) + DNN ranker (watch time)
 2018 MMoE / DPP   --- multi-gate experts; windowed DPP feed diversification
 2019 YouTube      --- two-tower + logQ | MMoE + shallow tower | REINFORCE | SlateQ
 2020 Google       --- DCN-V2, mixed negative sampling, ScaNN
 2021 YouTube      --- "valued watch time" from surveys; self-supervised towers
 2023 Google       --- TIGER: semantic IDs + seq2seq generative retrieval
 2024 YouTube      --- semantic IDs replace video IDs in ranking embeddings
 2025 YouTube      --- PLUM: LLM continued pre-training on SIDs, generative retrieval

Architecture (Das et al., WWW 2007)

 Google News CF (2007)

 click logs --MapReduce--> MinHash user clusters ---+
            --MapReduce--> PLSI (EM) user classes ---+--> Bigtable stats
 live clicks --> co-visitation counts (online) ------+         |
                                                               v
 request(user) --> candidate stories --> score = w1*minhash + w2*plsi + w3*covisit

Architecture (Davidson et al., RecSys 2010)

 YouTube 2010 (batch-computed, served from Bigtable)

 watch logs (24h sessions) --MapReduce--> co-visitation counts c_ij
                                        --> related(v_i) = top-N by r(v_i, v_j)
                                                  r = c_ij / f(v_i, v_j)
 user seeds S = {watched, liked, favorited, playlisted videos}
          |
          v
 candidates C_n(S) = union of related-graph neighbours up to n hops
          |
          v
 rank by linear mix of: video quality | user specificity | diversification
          |
          v
 top ~4-60 recs (pre-computed several times a day; served with explanation)

Architecture

      wide (memorization)                     deep (generalization)
 sparse ids + cross products         sparse ids -> embeddings (~32-d each)
            |                                  + dense features
            |                                     |
            |                               MLP (ReLU layers, e.g. 1024-512-256)
            |                                     |
            +----------- sum of logits -----------+
                               |
                            sigmoid -> P(app acquisition)

 serving funnel: query -> retrieval (ML + rules, ~O(100) apps) -> Wide & Deep rank

Architecture

 YouTube 2016

 corpus (millions) ---> CANDIDATE GENERATION ---> hundreds ---> RANKING ---> dozens
                        user history + context       |          rich features,
 other candidate  ----> -> MLP tower -> user vec u    |          impression data,
 sources (earlier       train: sampled softmax        |          weighted LR on
 work, co-vis, etc.)    serve: ANN top-N by <u, v_j>  +--------> watch time
                                                                     |
                                                       A/B test on watch time/user

Design

  query tower  q_i = f(user_i, context_i)          batch i = 1..B
  item tower   v_j = g(video_j features)
  logits       s_ij = <q_i, v_j> / tau  -  log p_j        (logQ correction)
  loss         weighted softmax CE over in-batch candidates (weight = reward)
  p_j          streaming estimate: hashed arrays of "last step seen" and
               moving average of the gap between occurrences; p_j ~ 1 / gap

Architecture

 YouTube Watch Next ranker (2019)

 query video + candidate + user + context features
                 |
          shared hidden layer (cheaper than feeding raw inputs to every expert)
                 |
      +-------- MMoE: N experts --------+
      |   gate_k(x) = softmax(W_k x)    |    (one gate per objective)
      +--+--------+--------+-----------+
         |        |        |
   engagement  engagement  satisfaction ... task towers
     (click)   (watch)     (like, dismiss, rating)
         |        |        |
         +--------+--------+--> logits
                               + shallow-tower bias logit (training only)
 position, device ---> SHALLOW TOWER ------^   (position dropout ~10%, approx.)
                 |
 serving: position feature missing -> debiased per-task predictions
          -> weighted combination (weights tuned by online experiments) -> rank

Top-K Off-Policy Correction for a REINFORCE Recommender (WSDM 2019)

 user event sequence (watches, context) --> RNN state s_t
                                              |
               +------------------------------+-------------------+
               v                                                  v
      pi_theta(a|s): softmax over               beta_hat(a|s): behavior-policy head
      millions of videos (deployed)             (gradient blocked into shared state)
               |                                                  |
               +---------- importance weight  pi / beta_hat ------+
                                     |
                lambda_K = K * (1 - pi(a|s))^(K-1)     (top-K correction)
                                     |
          REINFORCE update: sum_t  w_t * lambda_K * R_t * grad log pi(a_t|s_t)
          + weight capping, normalized IS, TRPO-style constraint

Architecture

 TIGER (Rajput et al., Google, 2023)

 item text/metadata -> Sentence-T5 (768-d, approx.) -> RQ-VAE
     level 1 code c1 (256 options)  -> residual r1
     level 2 code c2 (256 options)  -> residual r2
     level 3 code c3 (256 options)  [+ collision token c4]
 Semantic ID(item) = (c1, c2, c3, c4)

 user history: SID(i1) SID(i2) ... SID(in)  (+ user id token, hashed)
          |
   Transformer encoder-decoder (T5-style, small)
          |
   beam search decodes the next item's SID tuple -> lookup -> items

PLUM: Pre-trained Language Models for industrial-scale generative recommendation (2025)

 PLUM (Google / YouTube, 2025)

 1. Item tokenization: Semantic IDs v2
    multimodal content embeddings (+ co-engagement signal) -> RQ-style
    multi-level codes; tuned for coverage and low collision at YouTube scale
 2. Continued pre-training (CPT) of a pre-trained Gemini-family LLM
    on a mixture of: user watch sequences as SID tokens, SIDs aligned with
    text (titles, topics), so the model "speaks" SIDs and language
 3. Task fine-tuning: generative retrieval
    input: user context + history (SIDs + text) -> decode SIDs of next videos
 4. Serving: beam decoding of SIDs -> videos, as a retrieval source
    feeding the existing ranking stack

18. Netflix Case Study: From the Netflix Prize to a Foundation Model for Personalization

3.2 The blueprint

                      +--------------------------- OFFLINE ---------------------------+
   member events ---> | Hadoop / Hive / Pig: data prep, model training, batch scoring |
   (plays, ratings,   | -> trained models + precomputed results                       |
    browsing, search) +--------------+-------------------------+-----------------------+
        |                            | models                  | results
        |                            v                         v
        |               +---------------- NEARLINE ------------------+
        +-------------> | event-driven compute (e.g. on play event:  |
        |  (pub/sub)    | recompute user factors / scores),          |
        |               | results written to Cassandra / EVCache /   |
        |               | MySQL                                      |
        |               +---------------------+----------------------+
        |                                     | cached results / features
        v                                     v
   +------------------------------- ONLINE --------------------------------+
   | request (member, device, context) -> fetch precomputed + live signals |
   | -> light model eval / filtering / context adaptation -> page          |
   | strict SLA; FALLBACK to precomputed or unpersonalized results         |
   +-----------------------------------------------------------------------+

4.2 Approaches described by Netflix

  candidate rows (hundreds to thousands of possible row "themes"):
     genre rows (PVR) | Top Picks (Top-N) | Because You Watched (sims)
     Trending | Continue Watching | New Releases | My List | ...
        |      each row independently ranks its own titles
        v
  row relevance model  score(row | member, context, device)
        |
        v
  stage-wise greedy assembly, for slot k = 1..R:
       choose row maximizing  score(row | rows[1..k-1])
         - dedup titles already shown above (esp. in first visible columns)
         - diversity across row types and genres
         - constraints: e.g. Continue Watching high, row-type min/max,
           device-specific row counts and widths
        |
        v
  artwork + evidence selection per (member, title)   -> Section 5
        |
        v
  rendered page (top-m rows x top-n columns are what is actually seen)

5.3 Logging and offline evaluation

  page request -> for each title shown:
     artwork policy (contextual model + exploration)
        -> chosen image_id, propensity p
        -> impression log (member, title, image_id, p, context)
        -> member sees image -> quality play?  -> reward log
  offline:
     replay on randomized-exploration logs: keep events where the new
     policy picks the logged image; metric = take fraction (plays/impressions)
  online:
     A/B test confirms replay wins

7.1 The measurement funnel

  idea / new model
     |
     v
  OFFLINE  ranking metrics on held-out plays; replay / OPE on randomized logs
     |      (screening only: offline wins often do not transfer [4][18])
     v
  INTERLEAVING (stage 1 online)  team-draft, hours attributed to each ranker;
     |      approx. 100x fewer members than A/B for the same power [10]
     v
  A/B TEST (stage 2 online)  ABlaze cells; OEC = retention,
     |      sensitive proxy = streaming hours; proxy rewards validated here
     v
  launch -> long-term monitoring; quasi-experiments where no randomization

8. Consolidation: from many models to few

  BEFORE                                   AFTER
  canvas A -> model A (features A)         canvas A --+
  canvas B -> model B (features B)         canvas B --+--> one multi-task model
  canvas C -> model C (features C)         canvas C --+    input: (member, context,
  search   -> model S (features S)         search   --+     task/canvas id, query?)
  N pipelines, N on-calls                  1 pipeline, shared features, shared infra

9.2 Design (Chapters 5 and 6 summaries of [16], plus secondary sources [25][26])

  member event streams (plays, browses, searches, thumbs, My List, ...)
       |
       v
  interaction tokenizer: merge adjacent related events; attach attributes
       |  (sequence of tokens per member, lifetime history)
       v
  +-------------------- FOUNDATION MODEL (offline, large GPU training) ----+
  | item emb = gate(age) * ID emb + (1-gate) * metadata emb                 |
  | causal Transformer, sparse attention, sliding-window training           |
  | heads: next item | multi-token future items | genre / language aux      |
  +-----------+------------------------+-------------------------+----------+
              |                        |                         |
              v                        v                         v
       (1) direct use           (2) embeddings             (3) fine-tune /
       next-title scores        member + title vectors     subgraph in
       as features / ranker     -> embedding store          downstream models
              |                        |                         |
              +------------> rows, search, notifications, page gen <------+

10.2 Maestro

  Maestro (schedule / event trigger)
     -> Metaflow flow: fetch facts (Axion) -> build features (shared encoders)
        -> train (GPU, @resources) -> evaluate offline (replay/ranking metrics)
        -> publish model / embeddings (embedding store, aligned space)
     -> online services pick up new versions -> interleaving -> A/B (ABlaze)

19. Meta Case Study: From News Feed Ranking to Andromeda, Lattice, GEM and Generative Recommenders

 2014   GBDT + LR ads CTR (online joiner, NE metric)                       [16]
 2018   MSI ("meaningful social interactions") Feed value model            [3]
 2019   DLRM open-sourced; Instagram Explore 3-pass distillation funnel     [9][5]
 2020   Instagram constrained exploration; FB Search embedding retrieval    [6][37]
 2021   Feed ranking explainer; survey-weighted objectives; Neo/ZionEX      [1][2][12]
 2022   DHEN; DSI data-ingestion paper; TorchRec (RecSys)                   [17][15][13]
 2023   Lattice (ads model consolidation); Explore scaled to 4 stages      [22][7]
 2024   HSTU generative recs; Wukong; InterFormer; Andromeda   [30][19][20][25]
 2025   GEM ads foundation model; Lattice paper; DHEN for CVR               [27][23][18]
 2026   GEM trained at LLM scale (MFU 20-25%); ULTRA-HSTU                   [28][34]

1.2 Architecture: multi-pass ranking on "predictors"

  inventory: all eligible posts for the viewer (> 1,000 on average)
        |
        v
  pass 0: integrity processes + eligibility (remove violating / ineligible content)
        |
        v
  pass 1: lightweight model narrows inventory to ~500 most relevant posts
        |
        v
  pass 2: heavy multitask neural networks predict P(action_i) per post
          (like, comment, share, hide, "worth your time" survey answer, ...)
          -> combined into one value score per post
        |
        v
  pass 3: contextual re-ranking (e.g. content-type diversity, avoid runs of
          similar posts) -> final ordered Feed
                                                         (after Meta 2021 [1][4])

Ranking funnel.

  seed accounts --ig2vec KNN--> candidate accounts --> their media (+ other sources)
            |
            v
   ~500 candidates
            |   pass 1: distillation model (minimal features), trained to mimic the
            |           combined output of the later stages
            v
   ~150 candidates
            |   pass 2: lightweight neural network
            v
   ~50 candidates
            |   pass 3: full deep neural network (all dense features)
            v
   ~25 shown first (after integrity and diversity rules)
                                  (after Instagram 2019 [5]; counts approx.)

2.3 Explore 2023: four stages and "caching everywhere"

 +--------------+  <=1,500  +------------------+  top 100  +-----------------+  +--------+
 | Retrieval    |---------->| 1st-stage rank   |---------->| 2nd-stage rank  |->| Final  |
 | heuristics,  |           | two-tower,       |           | MTML network +  |  | rerank |
 | item-CF, PPR,|           | cached towers,   |           | linear value    |  | integ.,|
 | two-tower ANN|           | distilled from   |           | model (BO-tuned)|  | divers.|
 | (weighted)   |           | 2nd stage        |           |                 |  |        |
 +--------------+           +------------------+           +-----------------+  +--------+
   item tower: offline daily embeddings | user tower: online, fresh features
   some users' heavy-stage results precomputed off-peak
                                   (after Meta 2023 [7] and Explore system card [8])

7.3 The HSTU block

   X --> Linear --> SiLU --> split --> U, V, Q, K
                                       |
          SiLU(Q K^T + rab(position, time)) * V     <- no softmax; relative pos+time bias

20. TikTok/ByteDance and Kuaishou: Real-Time Short-Video Recommendation, from Monolith to OneRec

Online training loop.

 client actions (play, like, skip...) --> Kafka: action log --+
 serving-time feature log ------------> Kafka: feature log --+
                                                              |
                         Flink online joiner (join by request key;
                         in-memory cache + on-disk KV for late actions;
                         negative sampling)
                                                              |
                      +---------------------------------------+-----------+
                      v                                                   v
           Kafka: training examples                          HDFS (batch dumps)
                      |                                                   |
         online training workers <---------- same model code -----> batch training
                      |
         training PS (Cuckoo hash tables, admission + expiry)
                      |  sparse params: incremental sync of touched IDs (minutes)
                      |  dense params: synced far less often
                      v
         serving PS --> online inference for the feed

20.5.2 RankMixer (2025) and TokenMixer-Large (2026)

  raw features --> group/project --> T tokens x D
     [ token mixing (heads shuffled across tokens) -> add & norm
       per-token FFN / sparse MoE (separate weights per token) -> add & norm ] x L
  --> mean pool --> multi-task heads (finish, like, play time, ...)

20.5.4 The ByteDance pattern

       +-------------------- Monolith-style online training ------------------+
       | Kafka actions + feature logs -> Flink joiner -> streaming trainer    |
       | Cuckoo-hash sparse PS  --(minute-level incremental sync)--> serving  |
       +----------------------------------------------------------------------+
  request --> retrieval (two-tower / Deep Retrieval paths / multi-channel)
          --> pre-rank --> rank: LONGER (10k seq, KV cache) + RankMixer tokens
          --> value-model fusion (P_like*V_like + E_playtime*V_playtime + ...)
          --> diversity rules (no same creator / same sound back to back)
          --> For You feed
           (composite, synthesised from [1][4][6][11][12]; not an official diagram)

20.6.1 RecFlow and the six-stage funnel

  corpus (10^8+ videos)
     | multi-channel retrieval: i2i, u2i two-tower, KuaiFormer, ...
     v
  ~8,000 merged candidates
     | pre-ranking (lightweight)
     v
  ~500
     | cascade rank -> full rank (multi-task: pLTR, pWTR, pFTR, watch time, ...)
     v
  tens
     | ensemble sort (pXTR fusion) + re-rank (listwise, diversity, business rules)
     v
  ~6-10 exposed                                   (after [16][23][24]; approximate)

20.7.1 TWIN (KDD 2023)

  per behaviour j:  K_j = W * inherent_j        (precomputed, cached per item)
                    b_j = w . cross_j            (cheap, user-specific)
  per candidate c:  s_j = Q(c) . K_j / sqrt(d) + b_j
  CP-GSU: top-100 by s_j over ~10^4 behaviours -> ESU: multi-head target attention

20.9.1 OneRec V1 (February 2025 paper, June 2025 technical report)

 static feats -----+
 short seq (20) ---+--> Encoder (Transformer, RMSNorm) --> 405 context tokens
 pos-feedback(256)-+                                            | cross-attn
 lifelong (1e5) -> hier. k-means (2000) -> QFormer (128) -------+
                                                                v
                [BOS] s1 s2 s3 --> Decoder (causal self-attn + MoE FFN)
                                                                | beam search
                                                                v
                candidate sessions --> reward system:
                    P-Score (SIM-based, one tower per objective: CTR, LTR, VTR, ...)
                    + format reward (legal SIDs only) + SIR (viral down-weighting)
                                                                |
                IPA / DPO (V1 paper)  ->  ECPO = early-clipped GRPO (tech report)

21. Twitter/X Case Study: From WTF and GraphJet to the 2023 the-algorithm and the 2026 Phoenix Transformer

Architecture

            follow graph (in memory, Cassovary)
                         |
   user u --> personalized PageRank --> circle of trust (hubs, top-k)
                         |
            hubs  ==follow edges==>  authorities
                         |
                    SALSA iterations
                         |
      authorities ranked --> "Who to Follow" candidates --> filters --> UI

Production descendants

 Event Bus / Kafka (fav, RT, follow, client events)
            |
      recos-injector  (filter, combine, per-service topic)
            |
   +--------+---------------------+
   |                              |
  UTEG (GraphJet, 24-48h window)  other GraphJet recos services
   |
  input: viewer's RealGraph-weighted followees
  traverse user->tweet edges, aggregate engagement weight
   |
  top tweets with social proof "<followee> liked"

21.3.1 SimClusters (KDD 2020)

 follow graph A (consumers x producers)
        |  cosine sim over followers, threshold
        v
 producer-producer graph --MH community detection (k~145K)--> KnownFor V (20M x 145K)
        |
   U = A . V  ---> InterestedIn (consumer, long-term)
        |                        \
        |                         +--> producer embeddings (multi-community)
   fav stream (Heron) --> tweet vec += InterestedIn(faver)   [real-time]
        |
 SimClusters ANN: cluster -> top tweets index; user vec -> candidate tweets

21.4.2 Architecture

 client request (For You)
        |
  HOME MIXER (Scala, Product Mixer framework)
  ForYouProductPipelineConfig
   +-- ForYouScoredTweetsMixerPipeline  (mix tweets + ads + WTF users)
   |     +-- ScoredTweetsRecommendationPipeline
   |     |     candidate pipelines (fetch caps from ScoredTweetsParam):
   |     |       InNetwork (Earlybird)   <= 600
   |     |       TweetMixer / CR-Mixer   <= 400   (SimClusters, TwHIN ANN, ...)
   |     |       UTEG (GraphJet)         <= 300
   |     |       FRS (FutureGraph)       <= 100
   |     |     feature hydration (~6000 features)
   |     |     ScoringPipeline: heavy ranker (MaskNet, Navi)
   |     |     heuristics: author diversity, in/out balance, fatigue, dedup
   |     |     visibility filtering
   |     +-- ConversationService pipeline  (reverse-chron backup on failure)
   |     +-- Ads pipeline
   |     +-- WhoToFollow pipeline (FRS)
   +-- marshal -> timeline (modules, social context, cursors) -> client

21.4.6 The heavy ranker (MaskNet)

  ~6000 features (aggregates, graph, embeddings, content)
        |  double-norm-log featurization
        v
  +-----------+-----------+-----------+-----------+
  | MaskBlock | MaskBlock | MaskBlock | MaskBlock |   parallel
  | x*mask(x) | x*mask(x) | x*mask(x) | x*mask(x) |   (1024 each)
  +-----------+-----------+-----------+-----------+
        | concat (4096) -> MLP 2048 (shared)
        v
  10 task heads: MLP 256-128-1, sigmoid  -> P(engagement_i)
        |
  score = sum_i w_i * P_i   (weights from config)

21.6.2 Request path

 FOR YOU REQUEST
   |
 HOME MIXER (Rust) -- Post Pipeline (PhoenixCandidatePipeline)
   1 query hydration: user action sequence (recent engagements = main model input),
     following list, blocks/mutes, muted keywords, seen/served posts, topics
   2 candidate sources (parallel)
       in-network : Thunder (in-memory recent posts of followees)   cap 1200
       out-of-net : Phoenix retrieval (two-tower ANN)               cap 1000
                    SimClusters (cluster similarity, <=48h)
                    Popular posts (shared list)                      cap 500
   3 candidate hydration: text/media, author + labels, quoted post, lang, counts
   4 pre-scoring filters (dedup, 48h age, self, OON replies/RTs, seen/served,
     muted kw, block/mute, subscription, election filter, holdouts ...)
   5 scoring: PhoenixScorer -> RankingScorer (weighted sum, author decay,
     OON discount, new-author boost) -> VMRanker (DPP rerank)
   6 selection: TopKScoreSelector
   7 post-selection: VFCandidateHydrator -> VFFilter -> AncillaryVFFilter
     -> DedupConversationFilter
   |
 Blending Pipeline (ForYouCandidatePipeline): ads blender, WTF, prompts
   |
 side effects (after response): record served posts, refresh cache, log events

21.6.5 Phoenix ranking (transformer with candidate isolation)

  keys ->      | user | history (S) | candidates (C) |
  query user   |  v   |   v v v v   |   x x x x      |
  query hist   |  v   |   v v v v   |   x x x x      |
  query cand_1 |  v   |   v v v v   |   v x x x      |  <- self only
  query cand_2 |  v   |   v v v v   |   x v x x      |
  ...
  => score(c) depends only on (user, history, c): batch-invariant, cacheable

21.7 Visibility and Trust & Safety in 2026

 CONTENT UNDERSTANDING (async)            LABELING RULES             SERVE
 grox/ (LLM + classifiers: spam, adult,   scarecrow/ (event rules,   visibility-filtering/
   violent media, text/image embeddings)    botmaker rule engine)      per (post, viewer):
 media-model-proxy/, clip/, pnsfwmedia/   abuse-enforcement-svc/       ALLOW | NOTICE |
 agatha/ (blocks+reports vs favs)           (+ abuse-ledger appeals)   INTERSTITIAL | DROP
 bdsm/ (behaviour sequences)              safety-label-user-agg/       |
 user-cred-v2/ (PageRank cred)                     |                   VFFilter,
                \______________ labels -> storage _/                   AncillaryVFFilter

22. Pinterest Case Study: From Pixie Random Walks to PinFM Foundation Models

1. The home feed funnel at a glance

  user request
      |
      v
 +---------------------- CANDIDATE GENERATION (recall) ----------------------+
 | Pixie walks | learned two-tower EBR (Manas) | conditional / multi-emb EBR  |
 | token indexes | PinRec generative retrieval | explore/exploit sources      |
 +-------------------------------------+------------------------------------+
                                       | merge + dedup (thousands)
                                       v
                    Light-Weight Scorer (two-tower pre-ranking)
                                       | hundreds
                                       v
       Pinnability ranker (wide&deep + DCN-style crossing + MTL heads)
         <- TransAct / TransAct V2 sequence modules, PinFM, PinnerFormer
                                       |
                                       v
         Multi-Objective Blender (utility weights; PRL-PUTS per request)
                                       |
                                       v
                          home feed grid  (after [25][26][33])

Architecture

 user actions (saves, clicks)        offline graph build (daily)
         |                                   |
         v                                   v
 query pins Q = {q1..qk}, weights      pruned Pin<->Board graph (~120 GB)
         |                                   |
 +------ Pixie server (full replica, everything in RAM) ---------------+
 | per q: step budget ~ weight(q) scaled by degree(q)                  |
 |   walk pin -> board -> pin ... biased by user features,             |
 |   restart with prob alpha; count visits V_q[p]                      |
 |   early-stop when n_p pins have >= n_v visits                       |
 | combine: V[p] = (sum_q sqrt(V_q[p]))^2   (multi-hit boost)          |
 +-------------------------------+-------------------------------------+
                                 v
                    top-K pins -> ranking -> surface

Architecture and scale

  TRAINING (producer-consumer)
   CPU producers: sample (query, positive) pairs from co-engagement
                  -> random-walk neighbourhoods (top-T by visit count)
                  -> gather visual + text features            --+
   GPU consumer:  2 conv layers, importance pooling,             |
                  max-margin loss, random + curriculum negatives <+
  INFERENCE (MapReduce, no redundant computation)
   layer-1 embeddings for all nodes -> join -> layer-2 -> Pin embeddings
                  -> ANN index -> Related Pins / Homefeed candidates

Architecture

 user's recent actions (pin, action type, surface, time features)
     |  each pin: 256-d PinSage embedding (frozen) + learned feature embs
     v
 causal Transformer encoder
     v
 L2-normalised user embedding, recomputed daily for every user
     |
     +--> ANN retrieval against pin embeddings
     +--> feature in Homefeed ranking and in ads ranking models

8.2 TransAct: real-time actions inside the ranker (KDD 2023)

 real-time action stream                                candidate pin
         |                                                    |
 latest ~100 actions: PinSage emb + action type + timestamp   |
         |                                                    |
 early fusion: concat each action with candidate embedding <--+
         v
 small Transformer encoder
         v
 output compression (first K tokens + max pooling) -> flatten
         v
 concat with PinnerFormer + other features -> DCN v2 crossing -> MLP
         v
 multi-task heads (repin, click, hide, ...) -> utility

8.3 TransAct V2: lifelong sequences (2025)

 lifelong seq (~16k, int8)    real-time seq     impression seq
            \                      |                 /
             +-- candidate-anchored NN search (dot product in PinSage
                 space); most recent real-time actions always kept --+
                                   |
                     short selected subsequence
                                   v
                 single-kernel Transformer (Triton)
                                   v
       ranker heads  +  auxiliary Next Action Loss

8.4 PinFM: a foundation model for user activity (RecSys 2025)

 user history --> context Transformer (once per request) --> cached K,V
                                                              |
 candidates c1..c1000 --> crossing attention over cached K,V -+--> features
                                                                 -> ranker

10. GPU serving for ranking

 CPU era                  GPU era (2022+)            kernel era (2025+)
 tree/linear + MLP  -->   batched DNN, CUDA graphs -->  fused Triton kernels,
 per-candidate CPU        fused embeddings,            int8/int4 sequences,
 cost ~ model size        GPU preprocessing            KV dedup (DCAT)

11. Pinterest ads ranking

 ad candidates (offline/online ANN, targeting)
          v

          v

                   -> DCNv2 / ML-DCN crossing -> pCTR, long-CTR heads
          v

23. LinkedIn Case Study: Feed, PYMK, Jobs, LiRank, LiGNN, 360Brew and the ML Platform Beneath Them

23.1 The shape of LinkedIn's recommendation problems

  members (QPS = member count x interaction frequency)
        |
        v
  +-------------------+   large K (item pool >> member pool, e.g. posts),
  |  Retrieval layer  |   high recall, two-tower / GPU exhaustive / LLM dual-encoder
  +-------------------+
        |  hundreds to ~2,000 candidates
        v
  +-------------------+   small K, high precision; DCN-style rankers (LiRank),
  |   Ranking layer   |   or a text foundation ranker (360Brew)
  +-------------------+
        |
        v
  re-ranking / blending / fairness / ads slotting  -->  member-facing list

23.2.2 GLMix and GAME (Photon ML)

  GLMix / GAME (coordinate descent with offsets)
  ------------------------------------------------
  round r:
    1. fixed effect  f(x)       <- fit on ALL rows, offset = sum(random effects)
    2. per-member    g_m(x)     <- fit per member m on its rows, offset = f + h
    3. per-job       h_j(x)     <- fit per job j on its rows,  offset = f + g
  repeat until convergence
  serve: logit = f(x) + g_member(x) + h_job(x)

23.3.2 Venice: the derived-data online store

  Hadoop/Spark offline features --batch push--\
                                               >--> Venice store (per region,
  Kafka -> Samza nearline features --stream---/     active-active, CRDT merge)
                                                        |   read compute:
  Feathr defs (anchors, derived, registry)              |   dot / cosine
     |-- offline: PIT join onto impressions --> training data
     `-- online: batch-get features <---------- ranking services
                         (thin <10ms | fast <2ms | Da Vinci in-process <1ms)

23.4.1 Architecture (2019)

   FollowFeed FPR ----+   (network updates; >80% of feed items, 2019)
   Jobs FPR ----------+
   Recommended FPR ---+--> SPR: unified features, multi-objective score
   PYMK / modules ----+        sum_k w_k * P(action_k) (+ creator-side terms)
   Ads (separate auction) -------------+
                                       v
                      slotting (ads in allowed positions) -> Feed

23.4.4 Feed retrieval in the LLM era (2024-2026)

  2019:  FPR_1 ... FPR_n (per-inventory)  -->  SPR (GLMix/DNN)  --> blend
  2024:  + LiNR GPU out-of-network retriever; LiRank deep SPR; LiGNN embeddings
  2025:  LLM (LLaMA-3) dual-encoder retrieval, ~2k cands, quantized numeric tokens
  2026:  unified LLM retrieval + sequential generative ranker (approx., secondary)
         GPU "retrieval as ranking" + SilverTorch tensor ANN for pre-ranking

23.5.2 Architecture and key techniques

   sparse IDs --> embedding tables (compressed vocab, quantized)
   dense feats ----------------------------+
   member history seq --> Transformer -----+--> concat
                                           |
              +----------------------------+-----------------+
              |                            |                 |
        Residual DCN               Dense Gating          wide MLP
     (low-rank, attention,             |                    |
      residual cross layers)           |                    |
              +-------------+----------+--------------------+
                            v
                  multi-task heads (Feed actions / job apply / ad click)
                            v
                 jointly trained isotonic calibration layer
                            v
                calibrated P(action)  -->  value model / auction

23.6.1 LiNR: model-based neural retrieval on GPUs (2024)

  model binary = { query tower weights, item matrix [N x d], item attrs }
  request:  q    = tower(member)                       (GPU)
            mask = prefilter(attrs)                    (GPU, exact)
            s    = q . items^T over masked rows        (dense / quantized)
            topk(s) --> ranking

23.6.2 LiGNN: production GNNs (KDD 2024)

 heterogeneous graph (member/job/post/ad/company/skill, typed + timestamped edges)
     |  densify low-degree nodes (cold start)
     v
 temporal multi-hop sampling --> GNN (ID emb + content feats, long-term losses)
     |  7x faster: adaptive sampling, batch grouping/slicing, shm queue
     v
 embeddings (batch + nearline) --> Venice / ANN --> Feed, Jobs, PYMK, Ads models

23.7 People You May Know (PYMK)

  member m
    |-- 2-hop graph walk (friends-of-friends, weighted by edge strength)
    |-- co-attribute joins (same company/school/time overlap)
    |-- address-book / imported contacts (where consented)
    |-- GNN embedding ANN (LiGNN-style; covers cold-start members)
    v
  candidate union (dedupe, filter existing connections, blocked, pending)
    v
  ranker: P(invite) x P(accept | invite)  (reciprocity), plus growth objectives
    v
  diversification + frequency caps --> PYMK module (also slotted into Feed)

23.9.2 Formulation

 Instruction:  member profile + jobs + past interactions (applied / viewed /
               dismissed / no interaction); predict apply/view/dismiss for the
               "Question" job. (May include steering, e.g. "focus on skills,
               location, years of experience"; "30% weight to profile match,
               70% to historical activity")
 Member Profile:  current position, company, location
 Past job interaction data:
    applied: [Title, Location, Country, Company, Description] ...
    viewed:  [ ... ]
 Question:  Will the member apply to the following job: [ ... ]
 Answer:    The member will apply

24. Amazon and E-commerce: From Item-to-Item Collaborative Filtering to Query-Aware Retrieval

1.2 The algorithm

 user history / cart / page context
        |
        v
 +---------------------+     lookup per item      +---------------------------+
 | seed items (k)      | ------------------------> | similar-items table       |
 | purchased, rated,   |                           | item -> [(item, score)]   |
 | carted, viewed      | <------------------------ | precomputed offline       |
 +---------------------+   top-n lists per seed    +---------------------------+
        |
        v
 merge + aggregate scores, drop owned/seen items, apply business filters
        |
        v
 "Customers who bought this also bought", "Your Recommendations", cart recs

2.3 P-Companion: diversified complementary recommendation (CIKM 2020)

 query item q
    |
    v
 [product embedding (content + GNN over behaviour graph)]
    |
    +--> [complementary type transition]  type(q) -> {t1, t2, ..., tk}
    |                                       (diverse set of complement types)
    v
 for each ti: [type-conditioned item embedding] --> ANN within type ti
    |
    v
 merge per-type top items -> diversified "frequently bought together" list

3.1 Semantic Product Search (KDD 2019)

 query text ---tok--> [uni|bi|char-3gram|hashed OOV] --embed--> avg pool --+
                                                                          |
                         shared embedding table                          cos
                                                                          |
 product text -tok--> [uni|bi|char-3gram|hashed OOV] --embed--> avg pool --+
 (title, attributes)
                      offline: embed catalog -> ANN index; online: embed query -> kNN

3.3 Query-aware recommendation as a design pattern

           query q, user u, context (page, cart, locale, device)
                     |
     +---------------+-----------------+----------------------+
     |                                 |                      |
 lexical retrieval              semantic two-tower       item-to-item / complement
 (inverted index)               (query emb -> ANN)       expansion from top results
     |                                 |                      |
     +---------------+-----------------+----------------------+
                     v
       candidate union, filters (stock, delivery, region, policy)
                     v
       ranker: f(q, u, item, context) with ESCI-style relevance + behaviour labels
                     v
       slotting: exact matches first; substitutes; complements / "related" widgets

4.3 Architecture of a Personalize-based deployment

 clickstream / orders ----batch (S3 import)-----> datasets --> solution version (train)
         |                                                        |
         +--real-time PutEvents--> event tracker ------------+    |
                                                             v    v
 app request (user, item, ctx) --> campaign / recommender endpoint
                                    - candidate scoring (recipe model)
                                    - filters (stock, owned, category)
                                    - promotions / exploration slots
                                   <-- ranked item ids (+ scores)
 batch inference jobs --> S3 --> email, push, offline segments

6.2 Personalized embedding-based recommendations (2021)

 new listing --> item tower (title tokens, category, aspects) --> item emb --> ANN index
                                                                              ^

                                                                              |
                                          candidates --> downstream ranker --> module

7.2 Unified Embedding (KDD 2023)

                                                                         | cosine

                                         |
