                                                         --> learning-to-rank

10. A reference architecture for e-commerce recommendation

                         +---------------------------------------------+
  events (view, cart,    |  streaming layer: session features,          |
  purchase, search) ---->|  real-time user embedding, cart state        |
                         +---------------------------------------------+
                                     |
  request(user, page, query?, cart)  v
  ------------------------------> candidate generation (union)
          - item-to-item tables (co-view: substitutes; co-buy: complements)
          - two-tower ANN (content-rich item tower; sequence user tower)
          - query-conditioned semantic + lexical retrieval
          - complement-type expansion (type -> items)
          - popularity / trending / exploration slots
                                     |
                                     v
                business filters: stock, delivery region, store availability,
                already-owned, policy, merchant/seller constraints
                                     |
                                     v
                ranker: multi-task (click, add-to-cart, purchase, return?)
                features: user x item x query x context; price, deal, rating
                                     |
                                     v
                re-rank: diversity (by product type), dedupe variants,
                promotions, explanations ("because you bought X")
                                     |
                                     v
                widgets: "Frequently bought together", "Customers also viewed",
                "Buy it again", "Recommended for you", search results

25. Spotify and Audio Recommendation: Discover Weekly, BaRT, Podcasts, AI DJ, LLM Playlists, Deezer Flow, Pandora, and the Music Streaming Stack

1.3 Discover Weekly as a pipeline

  play logs, playlist co-occurrence (user-curated playlists as "documents")
        |                          |
        v                          v
  implicit MF / word2vec-style   playlist-track co-occurrence
  track & user vectors           (tracks that appear together)
        |                          |
        +-----------+--------------+
                    v
        candidate tracks near the user's taste vector(s)
        (ANN search: Annoy, later Voyager)
                    |
   filters: not already heard, artist diversity, explicit-content
   settings, cold-start audio-model candidates
                    |
                    v
         ranking + sequencing into a 30-track weekly playlist
                    |
                    v
           batch generation for every user, written to storage,
           served on Monday (precomputed, not real-time)

1.4 Content-based audio models for cold start

 audio (30s clips) -> mel-spectrogram -> conv layers (time-pooled)
                                             |
                                             v
                            predicted latent vector  ~=  CF vector (from ALS/LMF)
                                             |
          loss: MSE / weighted-MSE to the CF factors of tracks with enough plays
          use: embed tracks that have no plays -> same retrieval space

2.2 BaRT: Bandits for Recommendations as Treatments

          user context (time, device, history embeddings)
                         |
     candidate cards  x  candidate explanations ("Because you listened to ...",
     (playlists,          "Your top mixes", "Popular podcasts", ...)
      albums, shows)
                         |
                         v
          reward model  P(stream >= ~30s | user, card, explanation, ctx)
          (factorization machine with explanation interactions)
                         |
               epsilon-greedy policy  ---> log (action, propensity, reward)
                         |                         |
                         v                         v
                  rendered shelf            off-policy evaluation and
                                            retraining (IPS-weighted)

3.2 Generalized user representations (2024)

 Stage 1: content & interaction encoders           Stage 2: user aggregation
 ---------------------------------------           -------------------------
 audio / metadata / CF  -> item embeddings  ---->  user listening history (items,
 (per-entity pretrained)                           weighted by recency/engagement)
                                                          |
                                                          v
                                              autoencoder -> compact user vector
                                              (refreshed frequently, stored in a
                                               feature/embedding store)
                                                          |
                         +---------------+----------------+---------------+
                         v               v                v               v
                    Home ranking    search ranking   podcast recs    ads / other
                    (downstream models consume the general vector as a feature)

4.3 2T-HGNN for audiobooks (WWW 2024)

  heterogeneous graph:  audiobooks -- podcasts -- (co-listened / co-saved edges)
                        nodes carry LLM-based text embeddings of title/description
                               |
                               v
               HGNN (GraphSAGE-style message passing) -> item embeddings
                               |
  user features: music + podcast + audiobook  -->  user tower
  history, demographics                            |
                               +-------------------+
                               v
                two-tower (2T) model: user vec . item vec
                               |
                   ANN retrieval -> downstream ranking on Home / search

5.1 Spotify AI DJ (2023)

 user history -> recommender -> "segments" (theme + 4-8 tracks)  [precomputable]
                                      |
          editor-curated facts/ ----> LLM script generation (short, templated,
          style guides                checked/filtered before use)
                                      |
                                TTS voice model (Sonantic-derived)
                                      |
               client: [voice intro][track][track]...[voice transition][track]...
               user taps DJ button -> skip whole segment -> new theme

5.2 AI Playlist (2024) and prompt-based playlist generation

 prompt "upbeat 80s synthpop for running"
     |
     v
 LLM (fine-tuned seq2seq) --decode--> semantic IDs  e.g. <c12><c7><c3>  (per track)
     |                                   |  (IDs from clustering CF embeddings)
     |                                   v
     |                        ID -> track lookup (no fuzzy title matching)
     v
 personalization re-rank with user vector, dedupe, artist diversity, sequencing

6.2 Semi-personalized cold start (KDD 2021)

  existing users: embeddings u from MF on listening data
          |
          v
  k-means over warm users  ->  K clusters (each with precomputed top tracks)
          |
  new user registers: demographics (country, age), onboarding answers
  (selected artists/genres), first-day interactions
          |
          v
  feedforward network predicts the user's embedding u_hat (trained on warm users:
  input = their registration-time features, target = their eventual embedding)
          |
          v
  assign u_hat to nearest cluster -> serve that cluster's precomputed recs
          (semi-personalized: cheap, cacheable, robust to noisy u_hat)

7.1 Music Genome Project

 seed (artist/song) -> gene vector (expert-annotated, ~hundreds of attributes)
          |
          v
 candidate songs by weighted gene-space similarity
          |
 + collaborative signals from thumbs up/down across listeners (added over time)
 + per-station adaptation: thumbs on this station reweight genes/candidates
          |
          v
 ensemble ranker -> next song (licensing constraints: DMCA radio rules limit
                   skips and how often an artist/album can repeat)

10.2 Batch versus real-time split

  BATCH (daily/weekly)                          REAL-TIME (per request / per track)
  --------------------                          -----------------------------------
  MF / embedding training                       session context, last N tracks, skips
  audio & text content embeddings               user vector refresh (frequent)
  ANN index builds (Voyager/Annoy)              shelf/card ranking on Home (BaRT-like)
  Discover Weekly / Release Radar / Daylists*   radio / autoplay / DJ next-segment
  LLM explanation text precompute               bandit exploration + propensity logging

26. Marketplace and Travel Recommenders: Airbnb, Booking.com, Uber Eats, DoorDash and Others

26.2.1 Overall architecture

 guest query (place, dates, guests, filters)
        |
        v
 +--------------------------+     availability, price, geo filters
 | Retrieval                |---> (inverted index; since 2025 also
 |  - geo + availability    |      two-tower EBR with IVF clusters as filters [8][9])
 +--------------------------+
        |  candidates (order of thousands, approximate)
        v
 +--------------------------+     features: listing, query, guest, real-time
 | First-pass / base ranker |     session features from embeddings [1]
 |  DNN (pairwise/listwise) |     (journey ranker: multi-task base module [5])
 +--------------------------+
        |
        v
 +--------------------------+
 | Second pass / context    |     diversity given listings above [6],
 | twiddlers, business rules|     cancellation / rejection penalties [5]
 +--------------------------+
        |
        v
  ranked results  ---> logs (impressions, clicks, bookings) ---> training
                         \--> interleaving experiments [10]

26.2.4 Architecture, bias and cold start (KDD 2020)

   query + guest features            listing features (+ position at train time)
            |                                   |
     [ query tower ]                    [ listing tower ]
            |                                   |
       "ideal listing"  ----- distance -----  listing vector
                                |
                       score -> pairwise loss
   at serving: position feature := 0 ; new-listing engagement := neighbour estimate

26.5.1 Graph learning for dish and restaurant recommendations (2019)

 orders (per city) -> weighted bipartite graphs (user-dish, user-restaurant)
      -> GraphSAGE (sampled 2-hop, max-margin + low-rank positives), daily
      -> user / dish / restaurant embeddings
      -> similarity features ---> homefeed ranker ---> carousels and store list

26.5.3 Eats search pipeline: hydration is the hidden cost

 eater request
    |
    v
 retrieval paths (lexical, embedding/ANN, geo)  --> tens of thousands of candidates
    |                                                 (before the fix)
    v
 light hydration (ranking features only)
    |
    v
 first-pass ranker (high recall, low latency)
    |
    v
 second-pass ranker (multi-objective)
    |
    v
 full hydration (presentation data) only for shown results

26.6.3 Feature and prediction infrastructure

 events (orders, views, ETAs) --Kafka--> Riviera (Flink, real-time features) --+
 warehouse tables -----------> Fabricator (batch, declarative)  ----------------+
                                                                                v
                                                    online feature store (Redis)
                                                                                |
 homepage request --> candidate stores (geo, open now) --> Sibyl prediction -----+
                                                           (store ranking + UCB)
                                                                  |
                                                                  v
                                               carousels + store list on homepage

27. The Chinese Ecosystem: Alibaba, Tencent, Meituan, Baidu, Huawei, JD, Xiaohongshu and the Architecture of Hyperscale Commerce and Content

Overview

           THE CHINESE "FULL-LINK" RECOMMENDATION / ADS FUNNEL
   (Alimama's own description: recall -> pre-rank -> rank -> exposure
                 -> click -> conversion -> repurchase [12])

 corpus 10^7 - 10^9 items
   |  multi-channel recall: item-CF, EGES i2i, MIND multi-interest ANN,
   |  TDM tree beam search, RALM look-alike, graph walks (Graph-Learn / embedx)
   v  ~10^3 - 10^4 candidates
 pre-rank ("cu pai")  COLD: small DNN with cross features, compute-aware
   v  ~10^2 - 10^3
 rank ("jing pai")    DIN / DIEN / SIM / ETA / BST target attention;
   |                  ESMM / PLE / AITM multi-task heads (CTR, CVR, CTCVR, ...)
   v  ~10^1 - 10^2
 re-rank              PRM / PEAR / PIER / generator-evaluator permutations
   v
 exposure -> click -> conversion -> repurchase (labels flow back via streaming)

1.1 DIN: Deep Interest Network (KDD 2018)

 candidate ad emb --------------------------+
 behavior_1 emb --[act unit(b1, ad)]-- w1 --+
 behavior_2 emb --[act unit(b2, ad)]-- w2 --+--> sum_i w_i * b_i  (candidate-specific
 ...                                        |                       user interest)
 behavior_T emb --[act unit(bT, ad)]-- wT --+
        concat(user interest, profile, ad, context) --> MLP (Dice) --> pCTR

1.3 Lifelong sequences: MIMN/UIC, SIM, ETA

 UIC (event-driven)                      SIM (request-driven, two-stage)
 behavior event --> update memory        request(user, candidate c)
                    M_u in UIC             |  GSU: UBT lookup [user][cat(c)]
 request --> read M_u (O(1)) --> MIMN      |       (22 TB index, hard search)
                                           v  top-K behaviors
                                         ESU: multi-head target attn(c, top-K)
                                           v
                                         concat with short-term DIN/DIEN -> MLP

1.4 ESMM: Entire Space Multi-Task Model (SIGIR 2018)

 loss = sum_i CE(y_i, pCTR(x_i)) + sum_i CE(y_i AND z_i, pCTR(x_i) * pCVR(x_i))

  impression x --> [shared embedding layer] --+--> CTR tower --> pCTR ----+
                                              |                           (x)--> pCTCVR
                                              +--> CVR tower --> pCVR ----+

1.6 TDM and JTM: learned tree indexes for retrieval

 offline (XDL)                                  online (TDM Serving)
 item embs --k-means--> tree T_k                request(user behaviors)
     ^                     |                       |
     |      samples: ancestors(+), same-level(-)   v
     +---- train DNN M_k <-+                    level 1: score children of root (Blaze)
          (repeat until stable)                  keep top-K --> expand children
                                                 ... level L: top-K leaves = items
 export: tree.pb + dense/sparse/item_emb  --->   + business filters in Index layer

2.1 PLE: Progressive Layered Extraction (RecSys 2020 best long paper)

 level 1:  [VTR experts]   [shared experts]   [VCR experts]
              gate_A          gate_shared         gate_B
 level 2:  [VTR experts]   [shared experts]   [VCR experts]
              gate_A                              gate_B
           tower_A -> VTR (effective view, AUC)   tower_B -> VCR (completion, MSE)

7. Architecture patterns characteristic of Chinese hyperscale

         ALGORITHM-SYSTEM CO-DESIGN: WHERE THE WORK MOVES

  request-time compute  -->  moved to ...                 example
  ----------------------     ---------------------------  ----------------------
  attend over history        event-time memory update      UIC / MIMN [6]
  attend over 54K items      offline category index        SIM UBT, 22 TB [7]
  pick top-k behaviors       bit ops on SimHash codes      ETA [8], SDIM [59]
  embed images per sample    parameter-server compute      AMS / DICM [24]
  traverse graph n hops      write-time pre-sampling       DGS, 20 ms P99 [28]
  score 10^7 items           beam search on learned tree   TDM Serving [19]
  score m candidates         one pass, masked attention    MTGR [57]

Overview

           Common shape of a news / social / content recommender
  +----------------+     +--------------------+     +---------------------+
  | ingest stream  | --> | content encoders   | --> | item index (ANN /   |
  | (articles,     |     | (text, entities,   |     | inverted / time-    |
  |  posts, votes) |     |  images, author)   |     | bucketed store)     |
  +----------------+     +--------------------+     +----------+----------+
                                                               |
  +----------------+     +--------------------+                v
  | user activity  | --> | user encoder       | --> +---------------------+
  | (clicks, dwell,|     | (history attention |     | candidate sources:  |
  |  follows)      |     |  / RNN / counts)   |     | follow graph, ANN,  |
  +----------------+     +--------------------+     | trending, co-visit  |
                                                    +----------+----------+
                                                               v
                                    +---------------------------------------+
                                    | ranker (CTR/dwell/quality, recency,   |

Architecture and models

   offline (MapReduce)                    online (per request)
  +---------------------+         +-----------------------------------+
  | user click sets     |         | candidate stories (fresh)         |
  |  -> MinHash buckets |-------->| score = w1*minhash_cluster_clicks |
  |  -> PLSI classes    |         |       + w2*plsi_class_clicks      |
  +---------------------+         |       + w3*covisit(recent_clicks) |
  +---------------------+         +-----------------+-----------------+
  | click stream        |--updates-> cluster click counts / covisit   |
  +---------------------+         |  counts in a large KV store        |
                                  +-----------------------------------+

28.3.2 The model family: DKN, NPA, NAML, LSTUR, NRMS

 title words (<=30) -> GloVe 300d -> multi-head self-attn (20 heads x 20 dim)
                    -> additive attention (hidden 200) -> news vector r_i
 clicked news r_1..r_50 -> multi-head self-attn -> additive attention -> user u
 candidate news r_c  -> score = <u, r_c>
 training: 1 positive + 4 sampled negatives from same impression, softmax CE

"Hot" ranking for posts

score   = ups - downs
order   = log10(max(|score|, 1))
sign    = +1 / 0 / -1 by sign of score
seconds = epoch_seconds(post_time) - 1134028003
hot     = round(sign * order + seconds / 45000, 7)

Architecture

   user client                PDS                     Feed Generator (did:web)
  +-----------+  getFeed   +------+  getFeedSkeleton  +----------------------+
  | app view  | ---------> | PDS  | ----------------> | index built from the |
  |           |            |      |  (JWT signed by   | firehose; returns    |
  |           | <--------- |      |   user's key)     | [post URIs + reason] |
  +-----------+  hydrated  +------+ <---------------- +----------^-----------+
                 posts       hydrate: post content,              |
                             author, like/repost counts   com.atproto.sync.
                                                          subscribeRepos (firehose)

Trending: a chi-square-like spike detector

expected = accounts sharing the link one day earlier (min 1)
observed = accounts sharing it now
score    = 0                          if observed < 5 or expected > observed
         = (observed - expected)^2 / expected   otherwise
max_score kept with cooldown 2 days; decayed = max_score * 0.5^(age / 8h)
trend if decayed >= 1

What is verifiable

 Snap-style retrieval stack (composite of [24]-[26] and Chapter 2)
 +----------------+   +-------------------+   +-------------------------+
 | social graph + |-->| GiGL: subgraph    |-->| user/item/node embeds    |
 | engagement     |   | sampling, GNN     |   | -> ANN index (EBR)       |
 +----------------+   +-------------------+   +------------+------------+
 +----------------+   +-------------------+                v
 | item text/LLM  |-->| semantic IDs      |-->  generative retrieval (GRID)
 +----------------+   +-------------------+                v
                                              multi-source candidates -> ranker

                       WHERE THE LLM SITS (2023-2026)

  OFFLINE / NEARLINE (batch, cacheable)                     ONLINE (unchanged latency)

29.3.5 LinkedIn: LLaMA-3 dual-encoder retrieval for the Feed (2025)

 member profile + recent activity text --> LLaMA-3 (fine-tuned) --pool--> member vec
                                                                                |
 post text (nearline, on create/update) --> LLaMA-3 (shared or twin) --pool--> post vec
                                                                                |
                         ANN / GPU exhaustive search over hundreds of millions  v
                         -----------------------------------------> ~2,000 candidates
                                                                    -> Feed ranker

29.4.1 YouTube PLUM (2025)

 1. Item tokenization: Semantic IDs v2 (multimodal content emb + co-engagement)
       -> RQ-style multi-level codes, tuned for coverage and low collision
 2. Continued pre-training (CPT) of a Gemini-family LLM on:
       user watch sequences as SID tokens + SID<->text alignment (titles, topics)
 3. Task fine-tuning for generative retrieval:
       user context + history (SIDs + text) -> decode SIDs of next videos
 4. Serving: beam decoding of SIDs -> videos, as one retrieval source
       feeding the existing ranking stack

29.5 LLM as the ranker in production: LinkedIn 360Brew (2025)

 Instruction: predict apply/view/dismiss for the Question job (optional steering)
 Member Profile: position, company, location
 Past job interactions:  applied: [title, location, company, description] ...
                         viewed:  [...]   dismissed: [...]
 Question: Will the member apply to: [job text]
 Answer:   "The member will apply"   <-- score = logit(answer tokens)

29.6.2 InteRecAgent (Microsoft RecAI): LLM brain, recommender tools

 user: "something like Hollow Knight but co-op, under $20"
   |
   v

                                [item-sim: Hollow Knight]     -> candidate bus
                                [rank: preference(user profile)] -> top-k
   |                                                                 |
   +<------------------------ results (IDs -> titles) ---------------+
   v
 LLM writes the answer + explanation; profile memory updated (prefer/unwanted)

                    TEACHER (offline, expensive)

  +-------------+   +-------------------+   +----------------------+

  +-------------+   +-------------------+   +----------------------+
         \___________________ online serving at full QPS ______________/

29.10.1 Where the cost lands

 cost per day  ~=  (#requests) x (#candidates scored by LLM) x (tokens per candidate)
                   x (cost per token)                         <- per-request LLM ranker
               vs. (#items + #users refreshed) x (tokens) x (cost per token)
                                                              <- offline feature/embedding

30. Scaling Laws and Hardware for Recommenders: Compute, Embeddings, Accelerators and Cost

Overview

             parameters (log)                     dense FLOPs / example (log)
                 ^                                      ^
   2019-2022     |  ######## embeddings                 |  #  small MLPs
   "sparse era"  |  #        dense                      |     (memory-bound)
                 +------------------------>             +-------------------->
   2024-2026     |  ######   embeddings (still huge)    |  ########  attention /
   "dense era"   |  ####     dense (HSTU, GEM, OneRec)  |  ########  token mixing
                 +------------------------>             +-------------------->

3.1 Wukong (Meta, ICML 2024)

 embeddings X0 (n x d)
      |
 +----v-------------------------------------------+
 | Wukong layer i                                  |
 |   FMB: X_{i-1} X_{i-1}^T -> flatten -> MLP --+  |
 |   LCB: W * X_{i-1} (linear recombine) -------+--> concat -> LN + residual
 +------------------------------------------------+
      |  (repeat L times: orders 1 .. 2^L)
      v
 top MLP -> task heads

4.1 The sparse side: sharding, caching, offload

 sparse half (model-parallel)                dense half (data-parallel)
 +-------------------------------+           +----------------------------+
 | ID features -> sharded tables  |  all-to-  | interaction + MLP/attn     |
 | (TW/RW/CW, HBM + host offload)  |==> all ==>| replicated on every GPU    |
 | bandwidth- & capacity-bound    |  pooled   | FLOP-bound, all-reduce     |
 +-------------------------------+   embs    +----------------------------+
          ^ scale: memory, NICs                     ^ scale: tensor cores

5. Google TPU SparseCore: embeddings in silicon

 TPU v4 chip
 +--------------------------------------------------------------+
 |  TensorCores (MXU systolic arrays) <-- dense MLP / attention |
 |  SparseCores (dataflow tiles)      <-- embedding gather,     |
 |        |                                 pool, scatter-add   |
 |        +--> ICI (3D torus, OCS-reconfigurable) all-to-all     |
 |  HBM: shard of every large embedding table                   |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------+
   x 4,096 chips per pod (optically switched)

6.3 Andromeda and the mixed fleet

 Meta recsys fleet (as publicly described, 2024-2026)
   training: GPU clusters (ZionEX lineage -> large GPU clusters for GEM)
   retrieval: Andromeda DNN on Grace Hopper (unified memory) [18]
   ranking:  Lattice / GEM-distilled students on GPUs and MTIA [17][15][21]
   teacher:  GEM, LLM-scale, thousands of GPUs, MFU 20-25% [15][16]

8. Power, cost and datacenter footprint

 cost per served recommendation  ~=  (training $ / requests served over model life)
                                   + (serving FLOPs x $/FLOP at achieved MFU)
                                   + (embedding bytes x $/GB-month of the memory tier)
                                   + (network all-to-all at training scale)
 levers: MFU up | precision down | distill teacher->student | consolidate models |
         tier embeddings (SRAM/HBM -> DRAM/LPDDR -> SSD) | cache hot rows

9. Putting it together: a reference scaling stack (2026)

                    +-------------------------------------------+
  offline teacher   | Foundation recsys model (GEM / HSTU-scale) |  GPU cluster,
  (LLM-scale)       | long sequences, MoE, FP8, MFU 20-45%       |  power-law scaling
                    +---------------------+---------------------+
                                          | distillation, embeddings
            +-----------------------------+------------------------------+
            v                             v                              v
  retrieval (GPU + unified mem)   light ranking (CPU/GPU)       heavy ranking (GPU/ASIC)
  learned index, sub-linear       cheap towers, caching          RankMixer/Wukong-style,
  (Andromeda-like)                                               M-FALCON KV sharing
            \_____________________________|______________________________/
                                          v
             sparse substrate: sharded embeddings (TorchRec/TPU SparseCore),
             tiered memory (HBM -> DRAM/LPDDR -> SSD), INT8/INT4 rows,
             incremental checkpoints, row admission/eviction

31. Search Meets Recommendation: Learning-to-Rank, Personalized and Semantic Search, Hybrid Retrieval and Unified Models

31.1 The common skeleton

  explicit query (text, filters)     implicit context (user, session, page, geo)
             \                                 /
              v                               v
   +-------------------- QUERY UNDERSTANDING --------------------+
   | normalize, spell-correct, segment, classify (category,      |
   | intent, locale), extract attributes, rewrite / expand,      |
   | encode -> dense vector and/or sparse terms                  |
   +-------------------------------------------------------------+
              |                 |                    |
              v                 v                    v
   lexical / boolean     dense EBR (ANN)      behavior / graph sources
   (inverted index,      (two-tower, IVF,     (co-click, item-to-item,
    BM25, filters)        HNSW, SPANN)         history, trending)
              \                 |                    /
               v                v                   v
   +--------- MERGE: union / RRF / quotas / dedup (10^3 - 10^4) -----+
                                |
                                v
   first-pass ranker (GBDT/LambdaMART or small DNN, in-index or near data)
                                |
                                v
   second-pass ranker (deep multi-task model, personalization, cross-features)
                                |
                                v
   re-rank: diversity, business rules, ads blending -> page
                                |
                                v
   logs: impressions, clicks, add-to-cart, bookings -> labels, propensities

31.2.3 LambdaMART

  per query q, documents d1..dn with graded labels l_i
       |
       v
  current ensemble F_m(x) --> scores s_i
       |
       v
  for each pair (i, j) with l_i > l_j:
        rho_ij   = 1 / (1 + exp(sigma * (s_i - s_j)))
        lambda_ij = sigma * |delta NDCG(i <-> j)| * rho_ij
        lambda_i += lambda_ij ;  lambda_j -= lambda_ij
       |
       v
  fit regression tree m+1 to {(x_i, lambda_i)}
  leaf value = sum(lambda) / sum(second derivative)   (Newton step)
       |
       v
  F_{m+1} = F_m + eta * tree_{m+1}

31.4.1 Facebook Search: EBR inside the inverted index (KDD 2020)

  query text + searcher ctx --> query tower --> q (e.g. 128-d)
                                                 |
       Unicorn:  (nn q :radius r)  AND  (term:location ...)  OR  (lexical terms)
                                                 |
  index:  term = IVF centroid id ; payload = PQ(residual)
                                                 |
  hybrid candidate set --> ranking (embedding similarity also a ranking feature)

31.4.3 Etsy Unified Embedding (KDD 2023)

 query + user recent behavior --> query/user tower -----------+
                                                             | cosine
 listing text (transformer) --+                              |
 listing tokens (term emb)  --+--> product tower --> [v ; w * quality] --> ANN
 query-listing graph emb    --+                              |
                                                             v
                                lexical retrieval  --union-->  ranker

31.4.5 Airbnb: from embeddings-as-features to EBR

 guest query (place, dates, guests, filters) + guest context
        |
        v
 retrieval: geo + availability filters  AND  IVF cluster ids of query vector [17]
        |  (order of thousands of listings, approximate)
        v
 base ranker: two-tower / multi-task journey DNN, pairwise loss [14][15]
        |
        v
 second pass: diversity given listings above [16], twiddlers [15]
        |
        v
 interleaving for fast screening (~50x sensitivity vs A/B, approximate) [19]

31.5.3 Score fusion

  query --> [text() OR nearestNeighbor()]  on each content node
                 |
           first-phase: cheap expression (e.g. bm25 + closeness) on all hits
                 |
           second-phase: GBDT / small NN on top-N per node
                 |
   container: global-phase over merged top-K: RRF or normalize, cross-encoder
                 |
               results

31.7 Unified search and recommendation

 L1 shared infra      same ANN platform, feature store, ranking service
                      (Manas, Vespa, Unicorn)
 L2 shared space      one embedding space for queries, users and items
                      (OmniSearchSage, Etsy, Facebook unified embedding)
 L3 joint behavior    search history feeds recommendation and rec history feeds
                      search (UniSAR, Bing short/long-term)
 L4 one model         a single generative model serves both tasks
                      (Spotify GR study, Amazon SynerGen, Kuaishou OneSearch)

31.7.3 L4: one generative model for both tasks

  OneSearch-style unified generative search (simplified)

  query text --> keyword-aware encoding --+
  user behavior (search + rec history) --+--> encoder
                                          |
                                          v
                               decoder (beam search over
                               valid semantic-ID prefixes)
                                          |
             SID tokens:  [RQ1 4096][RQ2 1024][RQ3 512][OPQ 256][OPQ 256]
                                          |
                                          v
                    items  --> optional light re-rank --> results
  training: SFT on (query, user) -> purchased/clicked item SIDs,
            then preference / reward alignment

31.8 Reference architecture: a unified discovery stack

                       +------------------- shared feature store -------------------+
                       | user long/short-term seqs (search + rec), item stats,      |
                       | query stats, real-time session events                      |
                       +------------------------------------------------------------+
  request(surface, query?, user, ctx)
        |
        v
  query understanding (only if query): spell, classify, attributes, rewrite, encode
        |
        v
  retrieval portfolio (per surface quotas)
   - lexical / learned sparse (search only; also relevance guardrail)
   - unified two-tower ANN (query+user tower; empty query for feed)
   - item-to-item / graph (related items; expansion from top search hits)
   - generative SID decoding (optional, constrained)
        |
        v
  merge: RRF or learned blend; dedup; filter (availability, policy)
        |
        v
  first pass (in-index or near data): GBDT LambdaMART / small DNN
        |
        v
  second pass: multi-task DNN (click, purchase/booking, negative outcomes),
               surface-specific heads, position debiasing
        |
        v
  re-rank: diversity, ads, business rules  --> page
        |
        v
  logs + interleaving + A/B

Design

 per-user features (activity level, history, past response)
                 |
                 v
 +-------------------------------------------+
 | volume response model:                    |
 | predict E[weekly activity | k sends/week] |
 | and p(unsubscribe | k sends/week)         |

                 |
                 v
 +-------------------------------------------+

                 |  weekly budget k_u
                 v

33.2.2 Pinterest: unified visual embeddings

  training sets (per product task)     shared CNN backbone      unified embedding
  +----------------------------+
  | Lens (camera-to-Pin)       |--+
  | Shop-the-Look (product)    |--+-->  image -> CNN -> FC -> emb --> proxy softmax
  | Flashlight (in-image crops)|--+                              per task (training)
  +----------------------------+                    |
                                                     v
                                     serving: one (binarized) embedding per image
                                     used by every visual search product

33.2.4 Kuaishou OneRec: multimodal LLM item tokenizer

  title, tags, ASR, OCR, cover, frames
            |
     miniCPM-V-8B  ->  1,280 tokens  -> QFormer -> 4 vectors
            |                                   |
   item-pair contrastive (CF signal)     RQ-KMeans, 3 levels
   + title generation (aux)                     |
                                       semantic ID (c1, c2, c3)
                                                |
                         generative recommender decodes SIDs per session

33.3.1 Meta Feed: integrity first

  inventory (> 1,000 posts)
     |
  pass 0: integrity processes + eligibility   <- violating / ineligible content removed
     |
  pass 1: lightweight model -> ~500 posts
     |
  pass 2: multitask NNs -> P(like), P(comment), P(hide), P("worth your time"), ...
          -> one value per post
     |
  pass 3: contextual re-rank (diversity, avoid runs of similar posts)

33.3.3 X / Twitter: visibility filtering as a separate rule engine

 CONTENT UNDERSTANDING (async)          LABELING RULES              SERVE
 grox/  LLM + classifiers: spam,        scarecrow/ event rules,     visibility-filtering/
        adult, violent media,           botmaker rule engine        per (post, viewer):
        text/image embeddings           abuse-enforcement-svc/        ALLOW | NOTICE |
 media-model-proxy/, clip/,              (+ abuse-ledger appeals)     INTERSTITIAL | DROP
   pnsfwmedia/                          safety-label-user-agg/            |
 agatha/ (blocks+reports vs favs)                |                    VFFilter,
 bdsm/   (behaviour sequences)                   |                    AncillaryVFFilter
 user-cred-v2/ (PageRank credibility)            |
              \___________ labels -> storage ___/

33.4.2 YouTube: raters, borderline classifiers and authoritative boosts

  human raters (public guidelines)        survey responses (1-5 stars)
            |                                        |
   borderline / authoritativeness             satisfaction model
   classifiers (score every video)             (predict for every watch)
            |                                        |
            v                                        v
  candidate scoring:  value = f(engagement preds, satisfaction pred)
                      x demotion for borderline  x boost for authoritative
            |
   recommendation surfaces (Home, Up Next)  -- search and subscriptions treated separately

33.7.2 A reference policy layer

                 +-----------------------------------------------------------+
  scored         |  POLICY LAYER (config + rules, versioned, owned by policy) |
  candidates --> |                                                           |
  (p_engage,     |  1. hard eligibility: blocks, mutes, age gates, legal,    |
   p_negative,   |     jurisdiction (e.g. election filters)                  |
   labels)       |  2. value model: sum w_i * p_i, large negative weights    |
                 |     on report / block / see-less; survey-predicted value  |
                 |  3. demotions: borderline x a, low quality x b, OON spam  |
                 |     ineligible; boosts: authoritative x c                 |
                 |  4. diversity and fatigue: author decay, DPP              |
                 |  5. final visibility filter on top-K (+ ancillary items)  |
                 |  6. logging: every drop/demotion with reason, holdouts,   |
                 |     exploration share, per-account transparency report    |
                 +-----------------------------+-----------------------------+
                                               |
                                               v
                                          final page

34. Open-Source Frameworks, Tooling, Benchmarks and Public Datasets

              The open-source recsys stack (typical composition)
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Orchestration / compute     Metaflow (flows, versioning)   Ray (Data/Train/Serve)|
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Features                    Feast: offline store <-> registry <-> online store   |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Training                    TorchRec+FBGEMM | TFRS (+TFRA) | Merlin HugeCTR/Models|
|                             RecBole | MS Recommenders | LightFM | implicit       |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Retrieval index             FAISS | ScaNN | Milvus | Weaviate | Qdrant | Vespa   |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Serving / ranking           Triton (Merlin Systems) | Vespa phased ranking |     |
|                             TorchRec inference (C++/TorchScript)               |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Benchmarks / data           MovieLens Criteo Avazu Amazon KuaiRand MIND Yambda |
|                             BARS / FuxiCTR / RecBole protocols                 |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

34.1.1 TorchRec and FBGEMM (Meta / PyTorch)

        TorchRec sparse/dense hybrid step (per batch, conceptual)
  GPU0..GPUn each hold: replicated dense MLPs (DDP) + a SHARD of embedding tables
  1. input KJT (ids per feature) --all-to-all--> owner shard of each table/row range
  2. owner shard: fused table-batched lookup (FBGEMM TBE) -> pooled embeddings
  3. pooled embeddings --all-to-all--> back to the sample's GPU
  4. dense forward/backward (interaction + MLPs), allreduce dense grads
  5. embedding grads --all-to-all--> owner shard; fused optimizer update in-kernel
  Pipeline: step 1 of batch i+1 overlaps steps 2-5 of batch i

34.1.3 NVIDIA Merlin (NVTabular, HugeCTR, Models, Transformers4Rec, Systems)

  request(user_id)
     |
  QueryFeast(user features) --> PredictTensorflow(query tower) --> QueryFaiss(top-100)
     |                                                               |
     +--> FilterCandidates(remove already-seen) <--------------------+
                |
          QueryFeast(item features) -> UnrollFeatures -> PredictTensorflow(ranker)
                |
          SoftmaxSampling(temperature, top-10)  --> response
  Ensemble(...).export(dir);  tritonserver --model-repository=dir

34.3.2 ScaNN (Google Research)

 query q
   |
   v
 [1] partition (tree, num_leaves ~ sqrt(n)) -> pick num_leaves_to_search leaves
   |
   v
 [2] score candidates with asymmetric hashing (AH), anisotropic quantization,
     dimensions_per_block = 2   (or brute force, optionally int8-quantized)
   |
   v
 [3] rescore top k' (reordering_num_neighbors > k) with exact / int8 dot product
   |
   v
 top-k

34.3.4 Vespa

  query ---> container (stateless)
               | scatter
     +---------+---------+
     v         v         v
  content   content   content       per node: retrieve (WAND / HNSW / filters)
  node 1    node 2    node N          -> first-phase (cheap expr, all hits)
     |         |         |            -> second-phase (ML model, top rerank-count)
     +---------+---------+
               | gather top hits
               v
        global-phase rerank (e.g. ONNX cross-model on GPU) -> response

34.4.1 Feast

  event log (user_id, item_id, ts, label) --+
                                           |  point-in-time join
  feature tables (entity, ts, values) -----+--> training set (no leakage)
            |
            | materialize(-incremental)
            v
  online store (Redis/Dynamo/...) <-- get_online_features(entity rows) <-- ranker

34.6 Benchmarking practice

  Reproducible offline benchmark loop
  raw logs -> frozen preprocessing (versioned ID, e.g. Avazu_x4) -> temporal split
         -> library (RecBole / FuxiCTR / TorchRec) with fixed config + seeds
         -> full-ranking metrics (nDCG@k, Recall@k, AUC/logloss) + latency/memory
         -> compare vs simple baselines (popularity, ItemKNN, iALS, SAR)
         -> only then: online A/B (Chapter 12)

35. The Recommender System Design Interview Playbook: Feeds, PYMK, Ads, E-commerce and Notifications

35.1.2 The canonical architecture to draw first

                OFFLINE                                ONLINE (per request)
 +-------------------------------------+   +-------------------------------------+
 | logs -> label join -> training data |   | request(user, context)              |
 |   |                                 |   |   |                                 |
 |   v                                 |   |   v                                 |
 | train retrieval (two-tower, graph)  |   | candidate generation (parallel):    |
 | train rankers (MTL DNN)             |   |   ANN(user emb) | graph | trending  |
 |   |                                 |   |   | follows | rules  -> 10^3-10^4 |
 |   v                                 |   |   v                                 |
 | item embeddings -> ANN index -------+-->| filter: seen, blocked, policy       |
 | batch features -> feature store ----+-->| light ranker            -> 10^2-10^3|
 | stream features (Kafka/Flink) ------+-->| heavy ranker (MTL)      -> scores   |
 +-------------------------------------+   | value fn + re-rank (diversity, biz) |
                ^                          |   -> top K (10^1)                   |
                |                          +-----------------|-------------------+
                +--- impression / engagement logs <----------+

Architecture

 user_id + context
      |
      v
 +------------ CANDIDATE GENERATION (parallel, ~1-2K total) ------------+
 | two-tower ANN (recent watches) | co-watch / item-to-item | subs      |
 | trending / fresh (explore)     | search-history sources  | multi-int |
 +----------------------------------------------------------------------+
      |
      v   filter: watched, blocked, region/age policy, dedupe
 light ranker (distilled two-tower or small DNN)  ->  ~100-500
      |
      v
 heavy ranker: multi-task DNN (MMoE) -> p(click), E[watch time], p(like), p(share),
               p(dislike), p(survey satisfied); shallow position-bias tower
      |
      v
 value function  sum_i w_i * pred_i  ->  re-rank: diversity (DPP / MMR), freshness,
                                         creator caps, policy  ->  top ~10-20

Architecture: Facebook's four passes

 inventory: all eligible posts for viewer (> 1,000 on average)
   -> pass 0: integrity + eligibility filters
   -> pass 1: lightweight model -> ~500 posts
   -> pass 2: multitask NNs predict P(like), P(comment), P(share), P(hide),
              P("worth your time" survey) ... -> one value score per post
   -> pass 3: contextual re-ranking (content-type diversity, no runs of similar posts)

Architecture

 OFFLINE (daily / hourly)                          ONLINE
 graph snapshot at t ----------------------+      request(user)
   -> FoF expansion (2-hop) ~10^5-10^6     |        -> read precomputed PYMK list
   -> + EBR: GNN embeddings -> ANN         |        -> filter: already connected,
   -> + co-attributes: school, company,    |           blocked, pending, privacy
        contacts, location                 |        -> light re-score with fresh
   -> ranker: p(send) * p(accept)          |           signals (recent views)
   -> top ~100 per user -> KV store -------+------> -> diversity, frequency caps

Architecture

 ad request (user, placement)
   -> eligibility: targeting (age, geo, interests), budget/pacing, policy
   -> retrieval: tens of millions of eligible ads -> a few thousand      (Andromeda-like)
   -> light ranker -> hundreds
   -> heavy ranker: pCTR, pCVR, p(hide) heads; calibration layer
   -> auction: rank by bid * pCTR * pCVR + quality; price (GSP/VCG-style)
   -> blend with organic feed (ad load, spacing)
 logs -> online joiner (impression + click within window) -> streaming trainer

Architecture (from X's open-sourced pushservice)

 user id -> Target (eligibility, settings) -> query candidate sources
   -> hydrate candidates -> light filters
   -> LIGHT RANKER (pre-selects from the large pool)
   -> HEAVY RANKER (multi-task: p(open), p(engage), negative-feedback risk)
   -> take step: run expensive predicates (quality, fatigue) one candidate at a time
      until ONE passes -> send push + in-app notification

36. Classic Algorithms Foundations: Neighborhoods, Factorization, Linear Autoencoders, Embeddings, and Why Baselines Still Win

Overview

             The classic recommender family tree (simplified)

  Explicit ratings (Netflix Prize era)       Implicit feedback (clicks, plays, buys)
  ------------------------------------       ---------------------------------------
  user-kNN (GroupLens, 1994)                 item-to-item CF (Amazon, 1998-2003)
        |                                          |
  item-kNN (Sarwar et al., 2001) ---------------+  +--> SAR (co-occurrence + decay)
        |                                       |  |
  Funk-SVD / biased MF (2006)                   |  +--> SLIM (2011) --> EASE (2019)
        |                                       |               \        /
  SVD++ / timeSVD++ (Koren 2008-09)             |             linear autoencoders
        |                                       |
        +--> WALS / iALS (Hu-Koren-Volinsky 2008) --> iALS++ / revisited iALS (2021-22)
        +--> BPR-MF (Rendle 2009) --> WARP / LightFM hybrid (2015)
                                         |
  word2vec (2013) --> item2vec / prod2vec (2015-16) --> Airbnb listing emb. (2018)
                                         |
                       two-tower / sequential / generative models (later chapters)

1.2 Item-to-item CF (Amazon)

 OFFLINE (batch, periodic)                          ONLINE (per request, ms)
 ------------------------------------------------   -------------------------------
 for each item i1 in catalog:                        user context:
   for each customer c who bought i1:                  recent purchases / cart / views
     for each item i2 bought by c:                           |
        record co-purchase(i1, i2)                           v
   for each i2: sim(i1,i2) = cosine over customers    lookup top-N similar items
 store top-N similar items per item  ------------->   for each seed item
   (item -> [(item, score), ...])                            |
                                                             v
                                                      merge, de-dup, remove owned,
                                                      re-rank (business rules)

3.3 The ALS trick that makes it scale

 x_u = (Y^T C^u Y + lambda I)^(-1) Y^T C^u p(u)
     where  Y^T C^u Y = Y^T Y  +  Y^T (C^u - I) Y
                        ^^^^^     ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
                    precompute     only n_u nonzero rows (items u touched)
                    once, O(f^2 n)

5.2 EASE (Steck, WWW 2019)

 min_B ||X - X B||_F^2 + lambda ||B||_F^2   s.t. diag(B) = 0

 P   = (X^T X + lambda I)^(-1)                 # item-item, m x m
 B_ij = - P_ij / P_jj   for i != j ,  B_jj = 0
 scores(u) = x_u B                             # dense item-item "autoencoder"

5.2 EASE (Steck, WWW 2019)

  Linear item-item models: what each one learns

               similarity source           constraints            training cost
  item-kNN     cosine / Jaccard heuristic  top-k sparsify         O(nnz * avg deg)
  SAR          co-occurrence + rescale     time-decayed A         O(nnz + m^2)
  RP3beta      3-step random walk          popularity penalty     sparse mat-mults
  SLIM         learned (elastic net)       W >= 0, diag 0         m regressions
  EASE         learned (ridge)             diag 0                 one m x m inverse

6.3 Airbnb listing embeddings (KDD 2018 best paper)

 Airbnb-style session embedding pipeline
 --------------------------------------
  click logs --> sessionize (30-min gap) --> filter (dwell >= threshold)
       |
       v
  SGNS training (window, negatives = random + same-market, global ctx = booking)
       |
       v
  listing vectors (d=32) --> ANN index --> "similar listings" candidates
       |
       +--> real-time features: sim(candidate, recently clicked / skipped)
                                  --> fed to GBDT/NN search ranker

10. Reference architecture: classic models in a modern pipeline

                              +----------------------------+
   interaction logs --------> | batch training (daily)     |
   (views, plays, buys)       |  - item-kNN / RP3beta      |----> item->topN table (KV)
                              |  - iALS / BPR (Spark/GPU)  |----> item & user factors
                              |  - EASE (if m small)       |----> B matrix / top-N
                              |  - item2vec on sessions    |----> item vectors
                              +-------------+--------------+
                                            |
                                    ANN index build (Faiss/ScaNN/Annoy)
                                            |
 request --> context (recent items, user id) v
         --> candidate generation: union of
               [kNN lookups of recent items] + [ANN on user factor]
               + [content / trending for cold users]          (~100s-1000s)
         --> feature join (incl. CF scores, embedding sims as features)
         --> learned ranker (GBDT or DNN)                           (~10s)
         --> business rules, diversity, dedup  --> response

37. Real-Time and Session-Based Recommendation: Streaming Updates, Online Learning, Nearline Pipelines and In-Session Intent

37.1.1 Offline, nearline, online: the Netflix blueprint (2013)

 member events (plays, ratings, browsing, search)
        |
        +--> OFFLINE  (Hadoop/Hive/Pig): training, batch scoring; no data or
        |             complexity limit, but stale and context-blind
        |
        +--> NEARLINE (event-driven, no request waiting): e.g. on a play event
        |             recompute member factors and scores; results written to
        |             Cassandra / EVCache / MySQL
        |
        v
     ONLINE (per request, strict SLA): fetch precomputed + live signals,
            light model eval, filtering, context; FALLBACK to precomputed
            or unpersonalized results on timeout

37.3.1 Twitter's aggregation framework: one definition, two engines

            client events (Kafka client-event topics)
                 |                               |
   daily DataRecord dumps (HDFS)        Storm/Heron (summingbird)
                 |                               |
   batch aggregates -> Manhattan KV     real-time aggregates -> memcached
                 \___________________   _________________/
                                     \ /
              Home Mixer / pushservice feature hydration

37.4.1 GraphJet (Twitter, VLDB 2016)

 Kafka (fav, RT, follow, client events)
        |
  recos-injector (filter, combine, per-service topic)
        |
  UTEG / GraphJet (24-48 h window, all in RAM, full replica per server)
        |  input: viewer's weighted followees
        v
  tweets ranked by followee engagement, with social proof

37.5.1 Pinterest TransAct (KDD 2023)

 real-time action stream                                  candidate Pin
         |                                                      |
 latest ~100 actions: PinSage emb + action type + timestamp     |
         |                                                      |
 early fusion: concat each action with candidate embedding <---+
         v
 small Transformer encoder
         v
 compress (first K tokens + max pooling) -> flatten
         v
 concat with PinnerFormer + other features -> DCN v2 -> MLP
         v
 multi-task heads (repin, click, hide, ...) -> utility

Architecture.

 client actions --> Kafka: actions ---+
 serving-time feature log --> Kafka --+
                                      v
           Flink online joiner (join on request key; in-memory cache +
           on-disk KV for late actions; negative sampling)
                  |                                     |
          Kafka: training examples              HDFS (batch dumps)
                  |                                     |
          online training workers <--- same code ---> batch training
                  |
          training PS: Cuckoo-hash tables (frequency admission + expiry)
                  |  sparse: incremental sync of touched IDs (minute-level)
                  |  dense : synced far less often
                  v
          serving PS -> online inference

37.8 TikTok and the short-video case

     +---------------- short-video real-time stack (composite) ----------------+
     | client swipe/watch events -> Kafka                                      |
     |   |-> Flink features (counters, last-N actions) -> KV  -> ranker input  |
     |   |-> fresh-item / graph indexes (incremental insert)  -> retrieval     |
     |   '-> Flink joiner(serving feature log) -> online trainer               |
     |          sparse rows: minute-level incremental push -> serving PS       |
     |          dense weights: hourly or slower                                |
     | request: lifelong history (cached, may lag) + last-N actions (fresh)    |
     +-------------------------------------------------------------------------+

2. RAG — Architecture Diagrams

01. RAG Foundations and Taxonomy: From REALM and RAG to the Modular Reference Architecture

Architecture

 masked sentence x ──► Embed_input(x) ─┐
                                       │  MIPS over ~13M Wikipedia blocks
 Wikipedia blocks z ─► Embed_doc(z) ───┤  (index rebuilt asynchronously)
                                       ▼
                         top-k docs + null doc ∅
                                       │
                         p(z|x) = softmax(Embed_input(x)·Embed_doc(z))
                                       ▼
            knowledge-augmented encoder p(y|z,x) (BERT over [x ; z])
                                       ▼
            p(y|x) = Σ_z p(y|z,x) p(z|x)   ── gradient flows into retriever

Architecture

  x ──► DPR query encoder q(x) (BERT-base, trainable)
           │  MIPS (FAISS, HNSW) over 21M x 100-word passages
           ▼  document encoder d(z) FROZEN; index never rebuilt
   top-K passages z_1..z_K with p_eta(z|x) ∝ exp(d(z)·q(x))
           │
           ├─► BART-large( [z_1 ; x] ) ─┐
           ├─► BART-large( [z_2 ; x] ) ─┼─► marginalize over z
           └─► BART-large( [z_K ; x] ) ─┘   (per sequence or per token)
                                              ▼
                                              y

Architecture

 question + passage_1 ─► T5 encoder ─► H_1 ─┐
 question + passage_2 ─► T5 encoder ─► H_2 ─┤  concatenate [H_1 ... H_N]
        ...                          ...    ├──► T5 decoder cross-attends to all
 question + passage_N ─► T5 encoder ─► H_N ─┘        └─► answer
 (independent, linear in N)            (joint "fusion" happens only in decoder)

Architecture

 input (2048 tokens) split into 64-token chunks C_1 ... C_32
        │
        │  for chunk C_u: key = mean-pooled FROZEN BERT(C_u)
        ▼  SCaNN approx. kNN over key-value DB (value = neighbour N + continuation F)
  Ret(C_u) = k neighbours x (64 + 64 tokens)
        │
        ▼  small bidirectional encoder over neighbours (conditioned on C_u)
  ┌──────────────────────── decoder stack ─────────────────────────┐
  │ standard blocks: self-attn + FFN                               │
  │ RETRO blocks:   self-attn + CHUNKED CROSS-ATTENTION + FFN      │
  │   tokens of chunk u+1 attend to neighbours retrieved for C_u   │
  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

10. The end-to-end reference architecture

                   OFFLINE (ingest + index)                     ONLINE (query path)
 ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌───────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
 │ 1 INGEST │─►│  chunk   │─►│  enrich / │─►│   2 INDEX    │
 │ parse,   │  │ size,    │  │ contextual│  │ dense (ANN)  │
 │ clean,   │  │ overlap, │  │ -ize, add │  │ + sparse BM25│
 │ ACLs     │  │ structure│  │ metadata  │  │ + metadata   │
 └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └───────────┘  └──────┬───────┘
                                                    │
 user ─► query understanding ─► 3 RETRIEVE ◄────────┘
         (rewrite, HyDE,        hybrid top-N (e.g. N=150), filters
          route, decompose)          │
                                     ▼
                                4 RERANK  cross-encoder / late interaction
                                     │    top-K (e.g. K=20)
                                     ▼
                                5 GENERATE  prompt assembly, ordering,
                                     │      compression, LLM call
                                     ▼
                                6 CITE      span-level attribution,
                                     │      support checking
                                     ▼
                                  answer + sources ──► logs ──► EVALUATE
                                                         (faithfulness, recall)

02. Document Parsing and Chunking: From Pixels to Retrievable Units

Overview

 raw files                 parse                      structure                 index
+-----------+   +---------------------------+   +-------------------+   +----------------+
| PDF/scan  |-->| text layer / OCR / VLM    |-->| typed elements    |-->| chunker        |
| DOCX/PPTX |   | layout detection (boxes)  |   | (title, para,     |   | (size, struct, |
| HTML/XLSX |   | table structure recog.    |   |  table, figure,   |   |  semantic,     |
| images    |   | reading-order recovery    |   |  list, caption)   |   |  late, ctx)    |
+-----------+   +---------------------------+   | + hierarchy tree  |   +-------+--------+
                                                | + bbox/provenance |           |
                                                +-------------------+           v
                                       +-----------------------------------------------+
                                       | enrichment: heading path, page, bbox, doc     |
                                       | title, LLM context/summary/questions, entities|
                                       +-------------------------+---------------------+
                                                                 v
                                              dense index + BM25 index + doc store

Docling (IBM Research)

PDF page --> text-layer parse ----------------------------+
         \-> page image --> Layout (RT-DETR family) ------+--> assemble -> DoclingDocument
                        \-> OCR (auto/Tesseract/EasyOCR/  |    (reading     (body tree,
                             RapidOCR/macOS Vision/Surya)-+     order,       furniture,
                        \-> TableFormer (accurate/fast) --+     captions)    groups, prov)
                        \-> optional enrichers: picture classification,
                            chart extraction, formula/code

Reducto

page --> layout detect --> region router --+--> classic OCR (dense text)
                                           +--> VLM (tables, charts, complex)
                                           +--> ...
                         merge + verify <--+
                               |
                     agentic multi-pass correction --> blocks{type,bbox,text} --> chunks

Hierarchical and multi-granularity chunking

              doc
      +--------+--------+
   [2048]            [2048]            parent nodes (returned to LLM)
   /  |  \           /  |  \
 [512][512][512]  [512][512][512]      intermediate
  /|\                                  
[128][128][128] ...                    leaf nodes (embedded / searched)

AutoMergingRetriever: if most children of a parent are hit -> return parent

Anthropic Contextual Retrieval

for doc in corpus:
   cache(doc)                                 # prompt caching: write once
   for chunk in doc.chunks:                   # process sequentially to hit cache
       ctx = LLM("<document>{doc}</document> <chunk>{chunk}</chunk> situate it")
       text = ctx + "\n" + chunk
       dense.add(embed(text)); bm25.add(text)
query -> dense top-150 + bm25 top-150 -> rank fusion -> rerank -> top-20 -> LLM

Enrichment pipeline pattern.

chunk --> +heading path, page, bbox, doc title           (free, from parser)
      --> +entities, dates, doc type, ACL tags           (NER/rules; for filtering)
      --> +situating context / questions / summary       (LLM; for matching)
      --> embed( context + heading path + chunk )        (dense)
      --> bm25 ( context + chunk )                       (sparse)
      --> store( raw chunk, metadata, parent_id, prov )  (for generation + citation)

03. Embedding Models for Retrieval: Commercial APIs, Open Families, Compression and Adaptation

Overview

 INGEST (offline, batch)                         QUERY (online, per request)
+-------------+   +-------------------------+    +-----------+   +----------------------+
| chunk text  |-->| [doc prefix/task tag]   |    | user query|-->| [query instruction]  |
| (+title,    |   | "search_document: ..."  |    +-----------+   | "Instruct: ...       |
|  metadata)  |   +------------+------------+                    |  Query: ..."         |
+-------------+                |                                 +----------+-----------+
                               v                                            v
                  +-------------------------+                  +-------------------------+
                  | encoder (BERT-style or  |   same weights   | encoder                 |
                  | LLM backbone)           |<================>|                         |
                  | pooling: mean/CLS/EOS/  |                  |                         |
                  | latent-attention        |                  |                         |
                  +------------+------------+                  +------------+------------+
                               v                                            v
                  +-------------------------+                  +-------------------------+
                  | truncate to d' (MRL)    |                  | truncate to d' (MRL)    |
                  | L2-normalise            |                  | L2-normalise            |
                  | quantise: fp32/int8/bin |                  | (often kept fp32)       |
                  +------------+------------+                  +------------+------------+
                               v                                            |
                  +-------------------------+   ANN search (cos/dot/hamming)|
                  | vector index            |<------------------------------+
                  +------------+------------+
                               v
                    top-k candidates --> optional fp32/int8 rescoring --> reranker --> LLM

1.3 Voyage AI (voyage-3, voyage-3-large, voyage-4 family)

 shared-space asymmetric deployment (voyage-4 style)
 docs (once, offline) --> voyage-4-large  --+
                                            +--> same vector space --> index
 queries (per request) --> voyage-4-lite ---+        (MRL d', int8/binary)

2.1 BAAI BGE: from C-Pack to BGE-M3 and LLM embedders

                        +--> [CLS] -> dense vector (1024)      --- s_dense
 text -> XLM-R-style    |
         encoder (8192) +--> per-token weights -> sparse lexical --- s_sparse
                        |
                        +--> per-token vectors -> multi-vector  --- s_colbert
 hybrid score = w1*s_dense + w2*s_sparse + w3*s_colbert   (example weights 0.4/0.2/0.4)

2.3 Alibaba GTE and Qwen3-Embedding

 Qwen3 LLM (0.6B/4B/8B, causal attention)
          |
          v
 Stage 1: weakly-supervised pre-training on ~150M SYNTHETIC pairs
          (generated by Qwen3-32B: persona from Persona Hub x query type x
           difficulty x language, documents from Qwen3 pre-training corpus)
          |
          v
 Stage 2: supervised fine-tuning on ~7M labelled pairs + ~12M filtered
          synthetic pairs (keep if cosine sim > 0.7); InfoNCE with hard and
          in-batch negatives, false-negative masking
          |
          v
 Stage 3: model merging of SFT checkpoints (slerp)
          |
          v
 embedding = hidden state at final [EOS] token, MRL-capable, 32K context

3.3 Scalar (int8) and binary quantization

 query --embed fp32--> q
   |
   +--> binary(q) --Hamming top-40--> [binary index: ~5.2 GB, in RAM]
                                         |
                                         v
            load int8 vectors of the 40 candidates  [int8 store: ~47.5 GB, on disk]
                                         |
                       rescore: dot(q_fp32, doc_int8) --> top-10 --> reranker/LLM
 total ~5.2 GB RAM + ~52 GB disk  vs  ~200 GB RAM + ~200 GB disk for pure fp32

Reference pipeline.

 domain corpus chunks
        |
        v
 (1) synthetic queries: LLM writes 1-3 questions per chunk      [13][48][51]
     (or T5 query generator, as in GPL)                         [47]
        |
        v
 (2) hard-negative mining: retrieve top-k with base embedder/BM25,
     drop the positive and near-duplicates (false-negative risk)
        |
        v
 (3) optional pseudo-labels: cross-encoder scores (q, pos, neg) --> margins (GPL)
        |
        v
 (4) train: MultipleNegativesRankingLoss / Cached-MNRL (big effective batch)
            or MarginMSE (distillation), wrapped in MatryoshkaLoss
        |
        v
 (5) merge: average fine-tuned weights with base weights (soup/slerp)  [10][25][51]
        |
        v
 (6) evaluate on held-out REAL queries (NDCG@10, recall@k) vs base + reranker
        |
        v
 (7) re-embed corpus (or train query-side adapter only to avoid re-indexing)

04. Vector Databases and ANN Indexing: From In-Memory Graphs to Object-Storage-Native Engines

Overview

                 the generic vector-database data path (2024-2026 designs)
 writes                                                               queries
   |                                                                     |
   v                                                                     v
+--------+   +-----------------+   +------------------+   +-----------------------+
| router |-->| WAL / log       |-->| index builder /  |-->| immutable index files |
| (auth, |   | (object storage,|   | compactor        |   | (object storage)      |
| quota) |   |  Kafka, quorum) |   | (cluster, quant.,|   +-----------+-----------+
+--------+   +--------+--------+   |  graph build)    |               | lazy load
                      |            +------------------+               v
                      |  tail unindexed suffix          +-----------------------------+
                      +-------------------------------->| query node: RAM/SSD cache,  |
                         ("freshness layer")            | ANN + filter + merge + top-k|
                                                        +-----------------------------+

4.1.4 DiskANN / Vamana: graphs that live on SSD

 query --> entry point (near global centroid)
   loop:
     expand frontier using PQ codes held in RAM  (cheap approximate distances)
     pick best beam_width candidates
     read their SSD blocks (full vector + adjacency list in one I/O)
     compute exact distances, update candidate list
   until candidate list converges --> top-k
 knobs: max_degree, search_list_size, beam_width_ratio,
        pq_code_budget_gb_ratio, search_cache_budget_gb_ratio

4.2.4 Cost-based planners: the convergent design

            estimate |allowed| from inverted / payload / bitmap index
                                   |
          +------------------------+-------------------------+
          | tiny                   | medium                  | large (>~ 40-95%)
          v                        v                         v
   exact scan of allowed IDs  graph walk w/ allow-list   plain ANN + post-filter
   (payload index, bitmaps)   (+ACORN / extra edges)     (filter rarely bites)
          |                        |                         |
          +---------- fallback: if ANN returns < k, rerun exactly -------+

4.4.1 Pinecone serverless

 writers --commit--> blob storage + log (sequences all mutations)
                          |                         |
                 tail log |                         | tail log
                          v                         v
          +-------------------------+     +------------------------+
          | index builder           |     | freshness layer        |
          | geometric partitioning  |     | compact index over data|
          | (coarse->fine hierarchy)|     | newer than manifest's  |
          | immutable segments ->   |     | max indexed version    |
          | blob; update manifest   |     +-----------+------------+
          +-----------+-------------+                 |
                      | segments (lazy load + cache)  |
                      v                               v
          +--------------------------------------------------------+
          | query executors: load only relevant partitions of the  |
          | target namespace, merge with freshness results         |
          +--------------------------------------------------------+

4.4.2 turbopuffer

 client --API--> stateless ./tpuf query nodes (multi-tenant, Rust)
                 |  route by namespace for cache locality; any node can serve any ns
                 v
          +----------------------+   miss   +------------------------------------+
          | memory + NVMe cache  |--------->| object storage, prefix per         |
          +----------------------+          | namespace:                         |
                                            |  /wal/  one file per group commit  |
                                            |  /index/ centroids.bin,            |
                                            |          clusters-N.bin,           |
                                            |          exact filter + BM25 idx   |
                                            +------------------------------------+
 writes: buffer -> group commit (<= 1 WAL entry/s/ns) -> CAS -> async indexer
 reads (strong): indexed data + scan of unindexed WAL tail; ~10 ms consistency check

4.4.3 Milvus (Zilliz)

 SDK --> [ Access layer: stateless proxies (MPP reduce of partial results) ]
                 |
         [ Coordinator: exactly one active; DDL/DCL, TSO/time-tick,
           WAL<->streaming-node binding, query views, compaction/index tasks ]
                 |
   +-------------+-----------------+--------------------------+
   | Streaming node                | Query node               | Data node
   | shard "mini-brain": WAL       | loads sealed segments    | compaction,
   | consumption, growing-segment  | from object storage,     | index build
   | search, growing -> sealed     | serves historical data   |
   +-------------+-----------------+--------------------------+
                 |
   [ Storage: etcd (meta) | WAL (Woodpecker/Kafka/Pulsar) | object storage (S3/MinIO) ]

4.4.6 Chroma

 client -> Gateway (auth, quota, rate limit, logical plan)
              | writes                         | reads: rendezvous hash(collection_id)
              v                                v
          Log (WAL) ------------------->  Query executor (mem + SSD-cached indexes,
              |      consistent read        consults log tail for freshness)
              v
          Compactor: log -> vector/FTS/metadata indexes -> object storage;
                     registers new index versions in System DB (SQL catalog)

05. Hybrid Search and Reranking: Fusion, Learned Sparse, Late Interaction and Multi-Stage Cascades

Overview

                      query
                        |
         +--------------+---------------+------------------+
         v              v               v                  v
   +-----------+  +-----------+  +--------------+  +----------------+
   | BM25 /    |  | dense ANN |  | learned      |  | multi-vector   |   STAGE 1
   | inverted  |  | (HNSW,    |  | sparse       |  | (ColBERT/PLAID)|   recall
   | index     |  |  IVF)     |  | (SPLADE)     |  |  optional      |   1e6-1e9 docs
   +-----+-----+  +-----+-----+  +------+-------+  +-------+--------+   ~1-50 ms
         | top 100-1000 each            |                  |
         +--------------+---------------+------------------+
                        v
              +-------------------+
              | fusion: RRF or    |   STAGE 1.5: merge + dedupe -> 50-150 candidates
              | normalised linear |
              +---------+---------+
                        v
              +-------------------+
              | cross-encoder /   |   STAGE 2: precision, 50-150 pairs, ~50-500 ms
              | late-interaction  |
              +---------+---------+
                        v
              +-------------------+
              | LLM listwise      |   STAGE 3 (optional): top 20-100, seconds, $$
              | reranker          |
              +---------+---------+
                        v
              +-------------------+
              | threshold / top-k |   calibrated cut -> 5-20 chunks into the prompt
              +-------------------+

1.4 Where fusion runs

   Azure AI Search                         Elasticsearch / OpenSearch
   ---------------                         --------------------------
   text query --> BM25 (maxTextRecallSize   retriever tree:
                   default 1000)              rrf | linear
   vector q(s) -> HNSW (k each)                 |-- standard (BM25)
          \          /                           |-- knn (dense)
           RRF (k=60, per-query weights)         |-- sparse (ELSER/SPLADE-like)
             |  top 50 by default              text_similarity_reranker
           semantic ranker (top 50 only)          wraps any retriever,
             |  @search.rerankerScore 0..4        rank_window_size default 10
           response

   Vespa                                   Weaviate
   -----                                   --------
   content nodes: first-phase (all hits)   hybrid(query, alpha)
                  second-phase (100/node)    -> BM25F + vector in parallel
   container:     global-phase (100 total)   -> relativeScoreFusion (default)
                  RRF / normalize_linear        or rankedFusion
                  ONNX cross-encoder         -> optional reranker module

2.3 SPLADE architecture

  text --> BERT encoder --> MLM head: logits over |V| ~ 30k terms per token
                                  |
                          pool over tokens + saturate
                                  |
                       sparse vector {term: weight}   (most weights = 0, by FLOPS reg.)
                                  |
            +---------------------+----------------------+
            | documents: indexed offline in an           |
            |   inverted index (Anserini/PISA/Lucene/    |
            |   Qdrant sparse/Elastic, etc.)             |
            | queries: encoded online (or use a          |
            |   lighter/separate query encoder)          |
            +--------------------------------------------+

3.3 PLAID

 query tokens (32 x 128-d)
        |
        v
 [1] centroid interaction: score query tokens vs all centroids (K-means codebook)
        |   select candidate passages whose token-centroids score high (IVF probe)
        v
 [2] centroid pruning: drop low-scoring centroids; approximate MaxSim using
        |   centroid scores only (no residual decompression)
        v
