 [3] keep top candidates (FastPlaid default n_full_scores = 4096)
        |
        v
 [4] decompress residuals for survivors -> exact MaxSim -> final top-k

5.1 RankGPT: listwise permutation generation

  BM25 top-100:  [ 1 ............................................ 100 ]
  pass 1:                                              [81 ...... 100]  rerank 20
  pass 2:                                       [71 ...... 90]          step 10
  ...                                     (strong docs bubble forward)
  pass 9:        [ 1 ...... 20]  -> final order of the top 20
  => ~9 LLM calls per query, each with 20 passages in context

7.2 Vespa's phased ranking as a reference design

      +--------------------------- content node (x N) ---------------------------+
      | matching: WAND / weakAnd limits candidates to top-K per node             |
      |    |                                                                     |
      |    v                                                                     |
      | first-phase: cheap expression on EVERY matched hit (bm25 + closeness)    |
      |    |  rank-score-drop-limit can discard hits                             |
      |    v                                                                     |
      | second-phase: heavier expression (e.g., GBDT, MaxSim) on best 100/node   |
      |    |  total-rerank-count caps the sum across nodes                       |
      +----+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
           v merged
      +--------------------------- stateless container --------------------------+
      | global-phase: best 100 globally (rerank-count / ranking.globalPhase...)  |
      |   - cross-hit normalisers: normalize_linear, reciprocal_rank(k=60), RRF  |
      |   - ONNX cross-encoder inference, optional GPU (gpu-device)              |
      +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+

06. Query Understanding and Transformation: HyDE, Multi-Query, RAG-Fusion, Step-Back, Decomposition, Conversational Rewriting, Routing and Self-Query

Overview

                         QUERY UNDERSTANDING LAYER
  user turn + chat history
          |
          v
  +-------------------+   standalone question   +----------------------------+
  | 1. Contextualize  |------------------------>| 2. Route                   |
  | (condense/rewrite)|                         | (index / tool / effort /   |
  +-------------------+                         |  no-retrieval / model)     |
                                                +-------------+--------------+
                                                              |
              +-----------------------------------------------+
              v
  +---------------------------+     +---------------------------------------+
  | 3. Structure              |     | 4. Expand / transform                 |
  | query text + filter       |---->| multi-query | HyDE | step-back |      |
  | (self-query, implicit     |     | decomposition into sub-questions      |
  |  filters, top_k)          |     +-------------------+-------------------+
  +---------------------------+                         |
                                                        v
                         N retrievals in parallel (dense / BM25 / hybrid, filtered)
                                                        |
                                                        v
                     fuse (RRF / union + dedupe) -> rerank -> truncate -> generator

2.1 HyDE (Hypothetical Document Embeddings)

  query q --> LLM ("write a passage that answers q") x N samples
                    |  d1 ... dN  (hypothetical, may contain false facts)
                    v
  encoder f:  v = mean( f(q), f(d1), ..., f(dN) )      # official repo averages q + docs
                    |
                    v
  ANN search over real corpus embeddings --> top-k real documents

3.2 RAG-Fusion: multi-query plus Reciprocal Rank Fusion

                    +--> BM25(q)  ------+
  q ----------------+--> vector(q) -----+
  |                                     |
  +--> LLM: 4 diverse rewrites r1..r4   |
         each ri --> BM25(ri), vector(ri)--> 10 ranked lists
                                        |
                                        v
                      RRF: score(d) = sum 1/(60 + rank_i(d))
                                        |
                              pool of 50 candidates
                                        v
                    reranker (cross-encoder or LLM judge) --> top 5-10 --> LLM

4. Step-back prompting

  q ---------------------------------------> retrieve --> normal_context ---+
  |                                                                         |
  +--> LLM (few-shot: "paraphrase to a more generic step-back question")    |
         --> q_sb --> retrieve --> step_back_context ------------------------+
                                                                            v
                                     answer prompt(q, normal_ctx, step_back_ctx)

5.2 Framework patterns: answer individually vs. answer recursively

  PARALLEL ("answer individually")         SEQUENTIAL ("answer recursively")
  q -> LLM -> [s1, s2, s3]                 q -> LLM -> [s1, s2, s3]
        |      |     |                      s1 -> retrieve -> a1
       ret    ret   ret                     s2 + (s1,a1) -> retrieve -> a2
        |      |     |                      s3 + (s1,a1),(s2,a2) -> retrieve -> a3
       a1     a2    a3                      answer(q) = a3 (or synthesize)
        \      |     /
   synthesize(q, Q/A pairs)

5.4 Managed decomposition: Azure AI Search agentic retrieval

  retrieve(query, conversation history, reasoningEffort)
       |
       v
  [query planning LLM]  (skipped at "minimal")
       |  focused subqueries + knowledge-source selection
       v
  subqueries run in parallel over knowledge sources
  (keyword | vector | hybrid; indexed indexes or remote sources)
       |  each subquery semantically reranked (L2)
       v
  [medium only] semantic classifier (L3) judges sufficiency
       |  insufficient -> ONE follow-up iteration with revised plan
       v
  merged grounding content (+ optional references, activity log, answer synthesis)

7.1 Condense-question and history-aware retrievers

  history H = [(u1,a1), ..., (u_{t-1}, a_{t-1})],  follow-up u_t
        |
        +-- H empty? --yes--> retrieve(u_t)
        |
        no
        v
  LLM: rewrite(H, u_t) -> standalone q_t  -->  retrieve(q_t) --> answer(H, u_t, docs)
                                              (answer step still sees the raw turn)

8.2 Semantic (embedding) routing

  offline: route r -> utterances u_r1..u_rn -> encoder -> utterance index
  online:  q -> encoder -> sim(q, u_ri) aggregated per route
           best route score >= threshold_r ?  -> route name (static)
                                              -> route + LLM-extracted args (dynamic)
           else -> None (fallback pipeline / general LLM)

9.1 LangChain SelfQueryRetriever

  q --> LLM(query-constructor prompt: schema + attribute descriptions
           + allowed comparators/operators + few-shot examples)
     --> {"query": "teenager love",
          "filter": "and(or(eq(artist,'Taylor Swift'), eq(artist,'Katy Perry')),
                         lt(length,180), eq(genre,'pop'))",
          "limit": optional int}
     --> parse into StructuredQuery IR (Comparison / Operation / limit)
     --> store-specific Translator (Pinecone, Qdrant, Chroma, Weaviate, Milvus,
         Elasticsearch, OpenSearch, PGVector, MongoDB Atlas, Neo4j, Redis, ...)
     --> vectorstore.search(query, filter=native_filter, k=limit)

07. GraphRAG and Knowledge Graphs: Community Summaries, Graph Memory, and When Graphs Beat Vectors

Overview

  index-time LLM cost  ------------------------------------------------>  high
  |                                                                         |
  vector RAG     LazyGraphRAG   FastGraphRAG    HippoRAG/LightRAG    GraphRAG (standard)
  (embed only)   (NLP concept   (NLP graph +    (LLM triples or      (LLM entities +
                  graph, no     LLM community    entity/relation      relationships +
                  summaries)    reports)         profiles)            summaries + claims
                                                                      + community reports)
  query-time cost, global questions: high <-------------------------> low (pre-summarized)

1.2 Indexing pipeline

 documents
    |
    v
 [1] Compose TextUnits ---- chunk (default 1,200 tokens); unit of extraction + provenance
    |
    v
 [2] Document processing -- link documents <-> TextUnits; export Documents table
    |
    v
 [3] Graph extraction ----- LLM per TextUnit -> entities (title,type,description)
    |                       + relationships (source,target,description)
    |                       merge by (title,type) / (source,target)
    |                       LLM summarizes merged description lists
    |                       optional: claim extraction -> Covariates (off by default)
    v
 [4] Graph augmentation --- hierarchical Leiden community detection, recursive until
    |                       communities reach a size threshold -> multi-level hierarchy
    v
 [5] Community summarization -- LLM report per community (overview, key entities,
    |                            relationships, claims), then a shorter summary
    v
 [6] Text embeddings ------ TextUnit text, entity descriptions, report content
                            -> vector store (LanceDB default; Azure AI Search)
 outputs: Documents, TextUnits, Entities, Relationships, Communities,
          Community Reports, (Covariates)

1.3 Query modes

          query
            |
   +--------+---------+---------+          level-k community reports
   v        v         v         v          (batched into context-size chunks)
 [map]    [map]     [map]     [map]   --> rated points {text, score}
   \        |         |        /
    +-------+----+----+-------+
                 v
        filter/sort by score, pack to token budget
                 v
             [reduce] --> final answer (+ report/source references)

1.9 LazyGraphRAG: deferring all LLM work to query time

INDEX (no LLM)                         QUERY (budgeted LLM work)
--------------                         --------------------------------------------
docs -> chunks                         query -> LLM: 3-5 subqueries, expanded query,
   -> noun-phrase concepts                      refine with matching graph concepts
   -> co-occurrence concept graph      per subquery:
   -> graph-statistics optimization       best-first: rank chunks by embedding sim,
   -> hierarchical communities                       rank communities by top-k chunks
   -> chunk embeddings                    breadth-first: LLM sentence-level relevance
                                                  test on top-k untested chunks of
                                                  communities in rank order
                                          iterative deepening: recurse into
                                                  sub-communities after z empty
                                                  communities in a row
                                          stop: no relevant communities left or
                                                relevance-test budget / q reached
                                       map: concept subgraph -> group chunks by
                                            community -> LLM extracts claims
                                            -> rank, filter to context window
                                       reduce: LLM answers expanded query from claims

2.2 Architecture

INDEX                                          QUERY
-----                                          -----
chunks --LLM--> entities + relations           query --LLM (keyword role)-->
          (name, type, description;               low-level keywords  (entities)
           relation keywords/description)         high-level keywords (themes)
   |                                                 |                 |
   v                                                 v                 v
dedupe/merge into graph  <------------------  match entity       match relation
   |                                          vectors (local)    vectors (global)
   +--> KV store: entity/relation profiles           \               /
   +--> vector store: entity, relation, chunk         +--> 1-hop neighbourhood +
   +--> graph store: nodes/edges                      |    source chunks
   +--> doc-status store                              v
                                              (mix mode: + naive chunk vectors)
                                                      v
                                              [optional reranker] -> LLM answer

3.1 HippoRAG (NeurIPS 2024)

OFFLINE INDEXING                              ONLINE RETRIEVAL
passages --LLM OpenIE--> (s, p, o) triples    query --LLM NER--> query entities
                     |                                         |
                     v                          retrieval encoder: nearest KG nodes
          schemaless KG (noun-phrase nodes)                    |
                     +                                         v
   retrieval encoder (ColBERTv2/Contriever):    seed PPR at those nodes (node
   synonymy edges between similar nodes         specificity weighting)
   (example scripts: cosine >= 0.8)                            |
                     |                                         v
          node -> passage incidence matrix      PPR mass over nodes -> aggregate to
                                                passages -> rank -> LLM reader

4.1 Retrievers (neo4j-graphrag-python)

question --embed--> vector index on :Chunk(embedding) --top-k (node, score)-->
   retrieval_query:
     MATCH (node)<-[:PART_OF_CHUNK]-(e:__Entity__)-[r]-(nbr:__Entity__)
     RETURN node.text, collect(e.name + ' ' + type(r) + ' ' + nbr.name)
   --> context strings --> RagTemplate(context, question, examples) --> LLM

4.2 KG construction pipeline

            LEXICAL LAYER                         DOMAIN LAYER
(:Document)<-[:PART_OF_DOCUMENT]-(:Chunk)-[:NEXT_CHUNK]->(:Chunk)
                                    ^
                                    | [:PART_OF_CHUNK]
                              (:__Entity__:Person)-[:WORKS_AT]->(:__Entity__:Org)

Extraction granularity.

docs -> parse -> chunk (+lexical graph: Document/Chunk/NEXT_CHUNK)
     -> [sample] -> LLM schema proposal / prompt auto-tune
     -> per-chunk LLM extraction (structured output, schema-constrained)
     -> validate + prune (schema patterns, direction, empties)
     -> entity resolution (exact -> fuzzy/semantic -> LLM adjudication)
     -> write graph (batched) + embed nodes/relations/chunks
     -> optional: community detection (Leiden) + LLM reports
     -> optional: temporal validity on edges (valid_from / invalidated_at)

7.3 Decision heuristics

Is the question about the WHOLE corpus (themes, trends, "what are the main...")?
   yes -> community-hierarchy method: LazyGraphRAG (cheap index) or GraphRAG global /
          dynamic selection (precomputed reports, repeated queries on a stable corpus)
   no  -> Does the answer need >= 2 hops through shared entities?
            yes -> PPR graph (HippoRAG 2 / fast-graphrag) or vector entry + Cypher
                   expansion (Neo4j); DRIFT if a GraphRAG index already exists
            no  -> Is the domain inherently relational/typed with exact constraints
                   (org charts, tickets, products, regulations)?
                     yes -> property graph + Text2Cypher / curated KG (KAG, Neo4j)
                     no  -> hybrid vector + BM25 + reranker (chapter 05); add a graph
                            only when evaluations show multi-hop failures
Do facts change over time and must history be queryable? -> temporal KG (Graphiti)

08. Agentic and Iterative RAG: Self-RAG, CRAG, FLARE, IRCoT, Adaptive-RAG, ReAct, RL-Trained Search Agents and Agentic Search

Overview

  FIXED PIPELINE                                                      AUTONOMOUS AGENT
  |---------------|----------------|----------------|----------------|---------------|
  Naive RAG     IRCoT / DSP      FLARE / DRAGIN   Self-RAG / CRAG   ReAct, Search-R1,
  (1 retrieval) (loop fixed by   (retrieval fires (model or small   Claude Code,
                 the program)     on uncertainty)  critic grades    multi-agent research
                                                   and corrects)    (model owns the loop)
  control: code ------------------------------------------------------> control: policy
  cost:    1 LLM call + 1 search -----------------------------> 10s-100s of calls/searches

2.2 IRCoT: chain-of-thought as the query generator

  question q ---> BM25 (Elasticsearch) ---> paragraphs P0
       |
       v
  +---------------------------------------------------------------+
  | REASON: LLM(q, P_all, CoT so far) -> next CoT sentence s_i      |
  |   if s_i contains "answer is" or i == max_steps: stop          |
  | RETRIEVE: BM25(s_i) -> top-K paragraphs, add to P_all          |
  +---------------------------------------------------------------+
       |  (loop)
       v
  READER: answer from q + collected paragraphs (or read off CoT)

3.1 FLARE (Forward-Looking Active REtrieval)

  context y_<t ---> LLM drafts next sentence s'_t (no retrieval)
                         |
         all token probs >= theta ?  ---- yes ---> accept s'_t, continue
                         | no
                         v
        query = s'_t (the tentative sentence itself)
                         |
                retrieve (BM25 over Wikipedia / Bing)
                         |
        regenerate s_t conditioned on retrieved docs ---> continue

4. Self-reflective retrieval: Self-RAG

  x, y_<t --> [Retrieve?] --no--> generate y_t --> [ISUSE]
                  | yes (or P(yes) > threshold delta)
                  v
          Contriever-MS MARCO: top-K passages d1..dK
                  |
     +------------+------------+-------------+
     v            v            v             v      (parallel, one per passage)
   d1 -> [ISREL] y_t^1 [ISSUP] [ISUSE]   ...   dK -> ... y_t^K
                  |
     score_k = log p(y_t^k) + sum_G w_G * s_G(critique tokens)
                  |
     segment-level beam search (keep top-B) --> next segment

Architecture.

  query x --> retriever --> ~10 docs
                  |
     T5-large retrieval evaluator scores each (x, d_i) pair
                  |
     +------------+--------------------+-------------------------+
     | any score > upper threshold      | all scores < lower      | otherwise
     v                                  v                         v
  CORRECT                            INCORRECT                 AMBIGUOUS
  knowledge refinement:              discard retrieval;        both: refined internal
  decompose docs -> strips,          ChatGPT rewrites x to     + web external
  score strips, drop irrelevant,     keyword query -> web      knowledge
  recompose in order                 search API (prefers
                                     Wikipedia pages) ->
                                     fetch pages -> refine
                  \__________________________|_________________________/
                                             v
                                         generator

7. ReAct-style retrieval agents

  Thought: I need the castle David Gregory inherited.
  Action:  search[David Gregory (physician)]
  Obs:     "... inherited Kinnairdy Castle in 1664 ..."
  Thought: Now find how many storeys Kinnairdy Castle has.
  Action:  search[Kinnairdy Castle]
  Obs:     "... a tower house of five storeys ..."
  Action:  finish[five]

10. Multi-agent research: Anthropic's Research system

                         user query
                             |
                  LeadResearcher (Opus 4) --- saves plan to memory
                 /           |            \
         subagent 1     subagent 2  ...  subagent N   (Sonnet 4, own context window,
         web search     web search       web search    3+ parallel tool calls each)
                 \           |            /
                  condensed findings only
                             |
           lead synthesizes; loops if more research is needed
                             |
              CitationAgent attributes claims to sources
                             |
                       final report

11.1 Claude Code's design

  INDEX-FIRST (classic code RAG)          AGENTIC SEARCH (Claude Code)
  repo --chunk--> embed --> vector DB      repo (live filesystem, always current)
          (re-index on change)                    ^          ^          ^
  query --embed--> top-k chunks             grep/rg     find/glob    read ranges
          |                                       \          |          /
          v                                    model plans next probe from results
  LLM answers from k chunks                         (loop; subagent may summarize)
  + 1 round-trip, cheap                     + exact, fresh, no ingestion pipeline
  - stale, chunk boundaries, recall cap     - more tokens, more turns, slower

09. Long Context vs RAG: Cache-Augmented Generation, Prompt and KV Caching, Context Rot, and Context Engineering

Overview

   Section 1  Evidence: how models actually use long inputs
              (NIAH -> lost-in-the-middle -> RULER/NoLiMa/HELMET -> context rot
               -> Michelangelo/MRCR)
   Section 2  Long context vs RAG: head-to-head studies and hybrid routers
              (Self-Route, OP-RAG, LongRAG, Databricks, "RAG meets LC", LaRA,
               sufficient context)
   Section 3  Cache-augmented generation (CAG): preload instead of retrieve
   Section 4  Prefix / prompt caching: providers and serving engines
   Section 5  Non-prefix KV reuse for RAG: Prompt Cache, RAGCache, CacheBlend,
              TurboRAG, Block-Attention, EPIC, APE, KVLink; KVzip compression
   Section 6  KV-cache infrastructure: LMCache, Mooncake, llm-d, Dynamo
   Section 7  Context engineering for agents (incl. ACE playbooks)
   Section 8  Comparison table, decision guide, takeaways

Architecture.

            query + document
                   |
         +---------v----------+
         |  RAG step (top-k)  |   cheap: k chunks + query
         |  "Is this          |
         |   answerable from  |
         |   these chunks?"   |
         +----+----------+----+
     answerable|          |"unanswerable"
              v           v
         RAG answer   +---v-----------------+
                      | LC step: full doc   |   expensive, used only
                      | (whole context)     |   for the remainder
                      +---------------------+

3.1 Design

  OFFLINE (once per knowledge base)                ONLINE (per query)
  +-----------------------------+                  +--------------------------+
  | all documents D             |                  | query q                  |
  |   -> one long prompt        |                  |   -> append q tokens     |
  |   -> forward pass (prefill) |   KV cache C_D   |   -> decode using C_D    |
  |   -> save KV cache C_D -----+----------------->|   (no retrieval step)    |
  +-----------------------------+   (disk/GPU)     +------------+-------------+
                                                               |
                                    reset: truncate C_D back   |
                                    to its original length  <--+
                                    (drop appended q/answer)

4.1 The mechanism

  request N   [ tools | system | docs (static) | history | new user turn ]
  request N+1 [ tools | system | docs (static) | history | turn | new turn ]
               \______________ cache hit ______________/ \_ new prefill _/
  any byte change at position p invalidates everything after p

5.1 The problem

  Full prefill (correct, slow):     [sys][c1][c2][c3][q]  all tokens attend causally
  Naive concat (fast, wrong):       [sys][c1*][c2*][c3*][q]
                                         ^    ^    ^  each precomputed alone,
                                         |    |    |  position 0-based,
                                         +----+----+  no cross-chunk attention
  Fixes:  (a) re-rotate positions   (b) recompute a subset of tokens  (c) fine-tune the
          model to expect block-local attention  (d) add link/summary tokens

Architecture.

   retrieved chunks c1..cn                       query q
          |                                         |
   +------v---------------------+                   |
   | KV store (GPU/CPU/disk)    |  precomputed per  |
   | KV(c_i) computed alone     |  chunk, offline   |
   +------+---------------------+                   |
          | load (pipelined, layer by layer)        |
   +------v-------------------------------------------v------+
   | Layer l:                                                |
   |  1. fix positions of reused K (RoPE re-rotation)        |
   |  2. pick "high-KV-deviation" tokens: those whose KV     |
   |     would differ most under full prefill (selected in   |
   |     early layers, refined layer by layer)               |
   |  3. recompute KV only for those tokens (~10-20%) with   |
   |     full cross-chunk attention; keep the rest reused    |
   |  4. while computing layer l, load KV for layer l+1      |
   +-----------------------------+---------------------------+
                                 v
                           decode answer

6. KV-cache infrastructure

                  +-------------------------------+
   requests ----->| cache-aware router            |  hashes prefix blocks, looks up
                  | (llm-d / Dynamo / gateway)    |  global KV index, picks worker
                  +---+-----------+-----------+---+
                      |           |           |
                 +----v---+  +----v---+  +----v---+     prefill / decode pools
                 | vLLM / |  | vLLM / |  | SGLang |     (disaggregated or not)
                 | worker |  | worker |  | worker |
                 +----+---+  +----+---+  +----+---+
                      |  KV connector (LMCache / NIXL / Mooncake TE)
                 +----v--------------------------------------------+
                 | tiered KV store: HBM -> CPU DRAM -> SSD -> S3   |
                 +-------------------------------------------------+

7.4 Context-engineering reference architecture

  +-------------------------------- context window (per step) -------------------+
  | [tools: fixed set, masked]  [system prompt: stable]   <- cached prefix       |
  | [pinned knowledge / CAG corpus (optional)]            <- cached prefix       |
  | [compacted summary of older turns]                    <- re-cached on compact|
  | [recent turns verbatim] [recent tool results]                                |
  | [recitation: current plan / todo]  [new observation]  <- uncached tail       |
  +---------------------------------+--------------------------------------------+
            ^ select (JIT retrieval) |  write (notes, files)    | isolate
            |                        v                          v
  +---------+--------+   +--------------------+   +-----------------------------+
  | RAG / search /   |   | memory dir, files, |   | sub-agents with clean       |
  | code grep tools  |   | todo.md, NOTES.md  |   | windows -> condensed results|
  +------------------+   +--------------------+   +-----------------------------+

8.2 Decision guide

  Is the knowledge static and < ~effective context length of your model?
     |-- yes --> Is it queried repeatedly within the cache TTL?
     |             |-- yes --> CAG / provider prompt caching (pin it in the prefix)
     |             |-- no  --> long context per query, or small RAG
     |-- no  --> RAG.
                 Are the same chunks hot across many queries, and is TTFT critical?
                   |-- yes, can fine-tune --> TurboRAG / Block-Attention
                   |-- yes, stock model   --> CacheBlend via LMCache (or EPIC/APE)
                   |-- no                 --> standard RAG, cache only the static prefix
  Hard global / multi-hop questions?  --> route escalations to long context (Self-Route)
  Agent loop?  --> context engineering: stable prefix, JIT retrieval, compaction,
                   notes, sub-agents; track cache hit rate

10. Multimodal RAG: Visual Document Retrieval, Cross-Modal Embeddings, and Video and Audio Retrieval

Overview

                       MULTIMODAL RAG: FOUR RETRIEVAL FAMILIES
 source artefact                index representation                     generator
+----------------+   (1) parse/OCR/caption/ASR -> text chunks (+KG)   +--------------+
| PDF page       |---------------------------------------------------->|              |
| slide, chart   |   (2) render -> VLM -> 1 dense vector per page      |   VLM / LLM  |
| table, figure  |---------------------------------------------------->|  (page img,  |
| screenshot     |   (3) render -> VLM -> ~700-1,030 patch vectors     |   frames,    |
| video, audio   |---------------------------------------------------->|  transcript, |
|                |   (4) any modality -> shared omni-modal space       |   captions)  |
+----------------+---------------------------------------------------->+--------------+

Architecture

 OFFLINE (per page)                               ONLINE (per query)
 PDF -> rasterise -> 448x448 image                "which year has highest outlay?"
            |                                                 |
   SigLIP-So400m (32x32 = 1,024 patches)            Gemma tokenizer + LM
            |                                                 |
   Gemma-2B contextualisation (+ prompt)            linear proj -> n_q x 128
            |                                                 |
   linear proj -> 1,030 x 128 (bf16/fp32)                     |
            |                                                 v
   multi-vector store ---------------------->  MaxSim(Q,D) = sum_i max_j <q_i, d_j>
                                                              |
                                               top-k page IDs -> page images -> VLM

10.5 Serving Late-Interaction Visual Retrieval at Scale

 cost ~ (#pages scored) x (#vectors/page) x (bytes/vector) x (#query tokens)
          |                    |                  |
          |                    |                  +-- scalar int8 (4x), binary (32x),
          |                    |                      PLAID residuals [20][27][32]
          |                    +-- token pooling: hierarchical (3x), row/col mean (32x),
          |                        k-means [2][18][26]
          +-- multi-stage: MUVERA / pooled / dense prefetch -> full MaxSim rerank
              on 100-1,000 candidates [19][22][24]

Production patterns: Vespa, Weaviate and NVIDIA

 PDF/slide decks -> rasterise (1,500-2,500 px) -> page store (BLOB/object storage)
                         |
                         v
              Col* / DSE embedder (GPU)  -> [pooled|FDE|binary] ANN index
                                         -> full-precision multivectors (rerank store)
 query -> query encoder -> stage 1: ANN prefetch (100-1,000) -> stage 2: MaxSim rerank
       -> optional VL reranker / LLM-agent selection (top 5-10)
       -> VLM generator(page images + query) -> answer + page-image citations + heatmaps

RAG-Anything (HKUDS, 2025)

 PDF/DOCX/PPTX --MinerU--> {text | image | table | equation} + hierarchy
      text  ---------------------------------> chunks + entities --+
      image --VLM caption + spatial relations-> entity nodes ------+--> multimodal KG
      table --structured interpretation-------> entity nodes ------+    + vector index
      eqn   --LaTeX parse + concept mapping---> entity nodes ------+
 query -> vector search + graph traversal -> modality-aware rerank -> LLM/VLM answer

VideoRAG (HKUDS, 2025; KDD 2026): graph plus segment embeddings

 video --split 30 s--> segment_i
    |-- audio  -> Whisper ASR ------------------+
    |-- 5 frames -> VLM caption ----------------+--> text chunks -> entity/relation KG
    |-- frames  -> ImageBind 1,024-d -> visual vector index           + text vectors
 query --> {KG/entity search, chunk search, visual search} -> top segments
       --> re-sample 15 frames -> fine captions w/ timestamps -> LLM/VLM answer

11. RAG Evaluation: Metrics, Judges, Benchmarks, Golden Datasets and Production Monitoring

Overview

              reference answer / nuggets / gold doc IDs  (golden dataset)
                    |                 |                         |
                    v                 v                         v
 query -> [retriever] -> top-k -> [reranker] -> context -> [generator] -> answer+cites
   |           |                            |          |              |              |
   |   IR metrics: Recall@k, nDCG,  context precision  |   faithfulness/groundedness  |
   |   MRR, MAP (needs qrels / IDs) context recall     |   (answer vs context, NLI    |
   |                                (claims of ref vs  |    or LLM judge)             |
   |                                 context)          |                              |
   +---------------- answer relevancy (answer vs query) ---------------------------+
                     answer correctness (answer vs reference / nuggets)
                     citation recall / precision (each sentence vs cited passages)
                     refusal / negative rejection (when evidence is absent)

3.2 Architecture

   sample = {user_input, retrieved_contexts, response, reference?, reference_contexts?}
                                   |
               +-------------------+--------------------+
               v                   v                    v
     [claim extractor LLM]   [question generator]  [chunk relevance judge]
        response -> claims     response -> N qs     (chunk, ref|resp) -> 0/1
               |                   |                    |
     [NLI verifier: LLM or   [embed + cosine vs     [rank-aware AP over v_k]
      HHEM T5 classifier]     user_input]               |
               v                   v                    v
          faithfulness       answer relevancy      context precision / recall

5. ARES: trained judges plus prediction-powered inference

  corpus docs ----> [LLM few-shot query/answer generator] ----> synthetic (q, passage, a)
                                                                 + generated negatives
                                                                         |
                                                                         v
                                               [fine-tune classifier judges, one per
                                                dimension: ctx-rel, faithful, ans-rel]
                                                                         |
   RAG system outputs (unlabeled, large) ----------------------------->  | predictions
   human-labelled validation set (small, ~50 to several hundred) --------+
                                                                         v
                                   [Prediction-Powered Inference: correct judge bias using
                                    labelled set; output score + 95% confidence interval]

8. Citation accuracy

answer sentences s_1..s_n, each with citation set C_i
  for each s_i:
     recall_i = NLI( concat(passages in C_i) => s_i )            # 1 or 0
     for each c in C_i:
        irrelevant(c) = not NLI(c => s_i) and NLI(C_i \ {c} => s_i)
  citation_recall    = mean_i recall_i
  citation_precision = 1 - (#irrelevant citations / #citations)

9. Nugget-based evaluation and the TREC RAG Track

 topics --> [retrieval runs over MS MARCO v2.1 segments] --> pooled passages
                                                                  |
                                       [UMBRELA: LLM relevance labels 0..3]
                                                                  |
                       passages with judgment >= 2 --> [AutoNuggetizer: create nuggets]
                                                                  |  label vital / okay
 RAG answers (with [n] citations) --> [assign: support / partial / not] <-+
                                                                  |
                       vital_score, all_score, strict_* (full=1.0, partial=0.5)

12. Building golden datasets

  1. SEED              2. SYNTHESIZE               3. CURATE              4. FREEZE
+----------------+  +-------------------------+  +-------------------+  +---------------+
| prod query logs|  | KG over corpus (ragas): |  | SME review: keep, |  | versioned set |
| SME-written qs |->| nodes, NER, similarity  |->| fix, drop; label  |->| + slices      |
| support tickets|  | edges -> scenarios ->   |  | gold doc IDs,     |  | + judge cal.  |
| failure cases  |  | single/multi-hop,       |  | reference answer, |  | subset (PPI)  |
+----------------+  | specific/abstract qs    |  | nuggets (vital?)  |  +-------+-------+
                    +-------------------------+  +-------------------+          |
         ^                                                                       |
         +------------------ 5. GROW: add every production failure -------------+

            | retrieve span: query, chunk ids, scores, latency
            | rerank span

            v
     [trace store] --(sample 100%)--> cheap checks: HHEM/MiniCheck groundedness,
            |                         citation-NLI, refusal detector, PII, latency/cost
            |--(sample 1-10%)-------> LLM judges: context relevance, answer relevance,
            |                         completeness (pinned judge + prompt version)

                        +---------------------- offline / async ----------------------+

2.1 DoorDash: Dasher support with a two-tier guardrail and an LLM judge

                          |

              [retrieve: similar resolved cases + KB articles]

              [LLM: draft response from retrieved context]
                          |

     +--------------[LLM Guardrail]---------------+

            pass         |          fail

2.2 LinkedIn: a knowledge graph over support tickets

 historical tickets
   |  parse each ticket into a tree (rule-based + LLM)

   |  connect tickets: explicit links (clone/related) + implicit (embedding similarity)
   v

 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

          -> [embedding search finds candidate tickets]
          -> [LLM translates question to graph query (Cypher-like) over sub-graphs]
          -> [retrieved sub-graphs -> LLM answer]

 sources: Engwiki (internal wiki), internal Stack Overflow, engineering docs
     |  Spark ETL: scrape + chunk
     v

     |

     |

     |                                                          |
     |<----------------- answer + source links -----------------+
     v
 user feedback buttons (resolved / helpful / not relevant) --> metrics dashboard

Architecture.

 user query + page context (job post, profile, feed post)
     |
     v
 [Routing: small/fast LLM picks a specialized "agent"]  (job assessment, knowledge, ...)
     |
     v
 [Retrieval (recall-oriented): agent decides which internal APIs ("skills") to call:
   people search, job data, company data, external search; plus embedding-based
   retrieval of few-shot examples]
     |
     v
 [Generation (precision-oriented): larger LLM composes answer from noisy context]
     |
     v
 streamed response (all stages stream, to cut time to first token)

  at a finer level of the hierarchy]

13. Code Retrieval and RAG: Codebase Indexes, Repo Maps, and Agentic Search in Coding Assistants

Overview

                      CODE CONTEXT ARCHITECTURES (2023-2026)

  latency budget:   < 1 s (completion)      seconds (chat)        minutes (agent)
                    |                       |                     |
  INDEX-FIRST       Copilot/Cursor/Augment embeddings + sync ---> exposed as a tool
  LEXICAL ENGINE    Cody: Jaccard windows   Cody: BM25 over Sourcegraph search
  STRUCTURAL        tree-sitter intent      Aider repo map        LocAgent / RepoGraph
  AGENTIC SEARCH                                                  Claude Code, SWE-agent,
                                                                  SWE-grep subagents
                    <------------- convergence: hybrid toolboxes ------------->

13.2 Cursor: Merkle-tree sync, chunk embeddings, and teammate index reuse

  CLIENT (IDE)                                   CURSOR BACKEND
  files --> Merkle tree (SHA-256 per file,
            folder = H(children))  -- root/subtree hashes -->  server Merkle tree
                    | diverging branches only                      |
  changed files --> syntactic chunks --> embed (async) -----> chunk-embedding cache
                                                            (keyed by chunk content)
                                                                    |
  query --> embed ----------------------------------------> vector DB (obfuscated
                                                            paths + line ranges)
  local read of returned line ranges <------ top-k ids ----------+
  (code text is read from the client's own disk)

13.3 GitHub Copilot: a remote code-search index reconciled with the local diff

  query --+--> embed query (started eagerly)
          |
          +--> GitHub semantic code search @ indexed commit --> candidate chunks
          |                                                    (exclude diffed files)
          +--> local diff vs indexed commit
                  |-- > 2,000 files or > 70% changed --> skip remote, warn
                  |-- small diff --> local embeddings over changed files (15 s cap)
          merge --> ignore-filter --> sort by distance --> [optional reranker]
               --> top (token_budget / 250) chunks into the prompt

13.4 Sourcegraph Cody: from embeddings to search-engine retrieval and layered long context

  user query --> tokenize + cleanse --> Sourcegraph search over selected repos
                                         (up to 10 repos; adapted BM25 + learned signals)
                                                  | ranked file snippets
  IDE local context (open files) ---------------+ |
                                                v v
                                   global ranking across context providers
                                                |
                              take first N snippets (N depends on snippet length)
                                                |
                         prompt = prefix + user input + context --> LLM

13.6 Windsurf and Cognition: SWE-grep, an RL-trained fast-context subagent

  main agent (frontier model)            Fast Context subagent (SWE-grep)
  "where is X handled?" ------------->  turn 1: 8 parallel {grep, glob, read}
                                         turn 2: 8 parallel ...
                                         turn 3: 8 parallel ...
                                         turn 4: answer = [(file, line ranges)]
  reads only cited ranges <-----------  reward in training: F0.5(files, lines)

13.8 Aider: the PageRank-ranked repository map

  repo files --tree-sitter tags--> defs/refs per file (disk cache by mtime)
        |
        v
  file graph: referencer --(ident, w = mul * sqrt(#refs))--> definer
        |      mul: x10 mentioned, x10 long-name, x0.1 private, x0.1 common, x50 in-chat
        v
  personalized PageRank (chat files / mentioned names get personalization)
        v
  ranked (file, symbol) defs --binary search on count--> map within --map-tokens
        v
  prompt: repo map + files the user or LLM added to the chat

13.10 SWE-bench retrieval findings: what the evaluations say

  Evidence map (who found what)
  repo size / query type      | finding                              | source
  ----------------------------+--------------------------------------+-----------
  small repo, issue text      | grep/find sufficient; agent persists | [18][25]
  large repo (>1k files)      | +semantic search: +2.6% retention    | [2]
  any repo, latency-critical  | RL fast parallel grep subagent       | [19]
  issue -> function           | trained embed + rerank > agents/cost | [35]
  identifiers shared w/ query | BM25 ~ best dense (RepoEval)         | [39]
  NL issue -> code            | dense >> BM25 (SWE-bench NDCG)       | [39]

14. Answer Engines and Web-Grounded LLMs: Perplexity, Google AI Overviews and AI Mode, Bing/Copilot, ChatGPT Search, Search APIs for Agents, Deep Research and Citation Systems

Overview

                    THE ANSWER-ENGINE DESIGN SPACE
  latency      ~1 s            ~5-30 s                 minutes to tens of minutes
              |----------------|-----------------------|-------------------------|
  pattern     single-shot      agentic search          deep research
              grounding        (search inside the      (plan, fan out, read
              (1 query,        reasoning loop,         hundreds of pages,
              top results)     a few to ~10 calls)     write a cited report)
  examples    AI Overviews,    ChatGPT search,         OpenAI / Gemini / Claude
              Bing grounding,  Claude web search,      Research, Perplexity
              Sonar-style      AI Mode, Perplexity     deep research, Exa /
              answers          Pro search              Parallel / Tavily research

2. Reference architecture

 user query (+ conversation, location, locale)
      |
      v
 [1] TRIGGER / CLASSIFY ---- no search needed --> answer from parametric knowledge
      |  (is the answer current? niche? risky?)
      v
 [2] QUERY GENERATION: rewrite to standalone; generate 1..N queries ("fan-out")
      |
      v
 [3] RETRIEVAL: own index | search API | SERP provider     (filters: domain, recency,
      |                                                     locale, safe-search)
      v
 [4] CONTENT EXTRACTION: snippet | query-biased highlights | full page | PDF | live crawl
      |
      v
 [5] CONTEXT ASSEMBLY under a token budget (dedupe, per-URL caps, threshold filtering,
      |   code-based filtering)
      |
      +--> [6] AGENTIC LOOP? read results -> reflect on gaps -> new queries -> [3]
      v
 [7] GENERATION with inline citations (markers, spans, or post-hoc citation pass)
      |
      v
 [8] CITATION RENDERING + compliance (show sources, show queries, provider widgets)

15. RAG Frameworks and Managed Services: From Orchestration Libraries to Retrieval-as-a-Service

15.1 A reference model: who owns which layer?

 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | L5 ORCHESTRATION  agent loop / graph / pipeline; tools; memory; HITL; retries  |
 |    LangGraph, Haystack Pipeline+Agent, LlamaIndex Workflows, AI SDK agents,    |
 |    Agent Framework, DSPy modules (+ optimizer that compiles prompts/weights)   |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | L4 SYNTHESIS      prompt assembly, grounded generation, citations, guardrails  |
 |    Anthropic Citations, Bedrock RetrieveAndGenerate, Azure answer synthesis,   |
 |    OpenAI file_search annotations, Vertex grounding / check-grounding          |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | L3 RETRIEVAL      query rewrite/decompose, hybrid search, fusion, rerank,      |
 |                   metadata filters, thresholds                                 |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | L2 INDEX          vector store / inverted index / graph / SQL; ACLs            |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | L1 INGEST         connectors, parsing/OCR/layout, chunking, enrichment, embed  |
 +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

  Frameworks: thin over L1-L3 (adapters), thick at L5.
  Managed services: thick at L1-L4, increasingly reaching into L5 (agentic retrieval).
  Anthropic Citations: L4 only (bring your own retrieval).

16. RAG Security and Governance: Permission-Aware Retrieval, Injection, Poisoning, Privacy and Audit

Overview

                     THREAT MAP OF A RAG PIPELINE

  writers of     +-----------+   +----------+   +---------+   +-----------------+
  source docs -->| connectors|-->| parse /  |-->| embed + |-->| index / vector  |
  (incl. the     | + ACL sync|   | chunk    |   | metadata|   | store (+ ACLs)  |
  attacker)      +-----------+   +----------+   +---------+   +-----------------+
     ^  POISONING (16.4)   ^ stale ACLs (16.1)      ^ INVERSION (16.5)   |
     |  HIDDEN INSTR. (16.3)                        | cross-tenant (16.2)|
                                                                         v
  user --query--> [authn] --> [query + identity] --> retrieve --> top-k chunks
                                  |  ^                                  |
                                  |  +-- authz filter / ReBAC check ----+
                                  v                                     v
                            prompt assembly: system + user + UNTRUSTED CONTEXT
                                  |
                                  v
                               LLM --> tools / links / images / email (EXFIL)
                                  |
                                  v
                    answer + citations --> audit log / traces (PII in logs)

Design space: where the authorization check happens

  (A) PRE-FILTER (filter pushed into the ANN / BM25 query)
      identity --> resolve groups / authorized IDs --> vector_search(q, filter=ACL)
      + top-k is always k authorized hits     - needs ACL metadata on every chunk
      + nothing unauthorized leaves the index - large ID lists, stale on ACL change

  (B) POST-FILTER (retrieve broadly, then check each hit)
      vector_search(q, k = k * m) --> CheckBulk(user, hits) --> keep allowed --> top-k
      + uses the live authz system (fresh)    - recall loss if most hits are denied
      + no ACL copy in the index              - extra latency per query

  (C) SOURCE-DELEGATED (federated / query-time call to the source system)
      query --> connectors search *as the user* (OAuth on-behalf-of) --> merge
      + exact source semantics                - slow, rate-limited, no global ranking

3. Detection classifiers.

            CaMeL-style isolation applied to RAG (simplified)

  user query --> P-LLM (trusted) --> program:  docs = search(q)
                                               facts = Q_LLM.extract(docs, schema)
                                               reply(format(facts))
                                    |
                                    v
                   interpreter: every value tagged {source, readers}
                   policy: values derived from docs may NOT choose recipients,
                           URLs or tool names; send() requires readers ⊇ recipient
                                    |
            Q-LLM (no tools) <------+ sees untrusted docs, returns typed values only

16.5.3 PII redaction pipelines

  source --> [1 ingest-time detect + transform] --> index  (minimize what is stored)
  query  --> [2 query-time scan / pseudonymize]  --> LLM    (protect 3rd-party APIs)
  LLM    --> [3 output scan / deanonymize]       --> user   (stop leakage)
  traces --> [4 log redaction / retention]       --> SIEM   (logs are a datastore)

16.5.4 Embedding inversion attacks

   Vec2Text loop
   e* (stolen vector) --> hypothesizer --> x0 --> embed(x0)=e0
        ^                                               |
        +---- corrector(e*, x_t, e_t) --> x_{t+1} <-----+   (repeat ~20 steps, beam)

16.8 A reference architecture for a governed RAG service

 INGESTION PLANE                                             (write path = trust path)
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | connector (least-privilege, per-source)                                        |
 |   -> fetch content + ACLs + labels + version                                   |
 |   -> parse (hidden-text normalization) -> injection classifier (chunked 512t)  |
 |   -> PII detect/transform (hard-redact / label / vault)                        |
 |   -> chunk -> project ACL + label + source trust onto EVERY chunk              |
 |   -> embed (private model) -> encrypt vectors w/ tenant key -> tenant shard    |
 |   -> emit provenance record (doc id, version, hash, ACL snapshot id)           |
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 QUERY PLANE
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | authn (OIDC) -> principal + groups from token (never from request body)        |
 | -> tenant routing -> retrieve with ACL pre-filter -> post-check high-sens docs |
 |    via ReBAC -> robust aggregation / cluster filter for low-trust sources      |
 | -> prompt assembly: spotlighted untrusted context, minimal user-data mixing    |
 | -> LLM (injection-hardened) -> output scan (PII, verbatim-overlap, URLs)       |
 | -> egress policy: no auto-fetched images, link allow-list, confirm side-effects|
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 GOVERNANCE PLANE
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 | OTel GenAI traces (refs not content) -> immutable audit store (per tenant)     |
 | revocation SLO monitors | CI red-team suites | erasure cascade (doc->chunk->   |
 | vector->cache->trace) | label-based model routing (internal vs external LLM)   |
 +---------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

17. Fine-tuning vs RAG and Training the Retrieval Stack

17.1.7 Synthesis

                Does the model need NEW FACTS, or a NEW SKILL?
                               |
           +-------------------+---------------------+
           | facts (change often / need             | skill (format, reading,
           | citations / access control)            | domain jargon, ranking)
           v                                         v
   RAG + better retrieval                    fine-tune a component
   (train retriever/reranker,                  |
    sec. 17.3-17.6)                            +-- generator reads docs badly
           |                                   |      -> RAFT / RA-DIT LM-ft
           | facts static, corpus small,       +-- retriever misses domain intent
           | latency-critical, offline?        |      -> synthetic queries + negatives
           v                                   +-- ranking quality / latency
   consider synthetic CPT (EntiGraph)          |      -> distil LLM -> cross-encoder
   AND keep RAG on top [10]                    +-- retriever != what the LM needs
                                                      -> LM-supervised retrieval (LSR)

17.2.1 RAFT: Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (UC Berkeley, 2024)

 P fraction of examples:      Q + D* + D_1 ... D_{k-1}  -->  A* (CoT with quotes)
 (1-P) fraction of examples:  Q + D_1 ... D_k (no oracle) -->  A*

 A* = reasoning that quotes the oracle verbatim between
      ##begin_quote## ... ##end_quote## markers, then the final answer
 default: 1 oracle + 4 distractors; CoT generated by GPT-4-1106

17.2.2 RA-DIT: Retrieval-Augmented Dual Instruction Tuning (Meta FAIR, ICLR 2024)

            +--------------------- LM-ft ----------------------+
 instruction|  for each (x, y) and each of top-3 chunks c:      |
 dataset    |    minimise -log p_LM(y | "Background: c" + x)    |
 (20 sets)  |  one training instance per chunk                  |
            +---------------------------------------------------+
            +--------------------- R-ft -----------------------+
            |  LSR target: p_LSR(c|x,y) ~ exp(p_LM(y|c+x)/tau)  |
            |  minimise KL(p_R(c|x) || p_LSR(c|x,y)) over top-10 |
            |  update QUERY encoder only (index stays valid)    |
            +---------------------------------------------------+
 inference: top-10 chunks -> 10 parallel prompts -> weighted ensemble

17.2.3 ChatQA (NVIDIA, 2024): context-enhanced instruction tuning plus a tuned conversational retriever

 Stage 1: general SFT (about 128K instruction + dialog samples)
          -> conversational, but weak at context-grounded QA
 Stage 2: context-enhanced instruction tuning on a blend of
          - HumanAnnotatedConvQA (about 7k dialogues)
          - SyntheticConvQA (about 7k dialogues from GPT-3.5-Turbo)
          - single-turn context QA (DROP, NarrativeQA, ...)
          - a slice of stage-1 data (to keep instruction following)
          - about 1.5k turns annotated "unanswerable"
 Retriever: dense retriever fine-tuned on multi-turn conversational QA,
            taking dialogue history + current turn as the query

17.3.4 Reinforcement learning on retrieval outcomes (2025)

  question --> policy LLM --(query)--> frozen search engine / retriever
                   ^                           |
                   |                       top-k docs
                   |                           v
             PPO / GRPO update <-- reward: answer EM (Search-R1)
                                   or recall / nDCG (DeepRetrieval)

17.4.1 InPars v1 and v2 (NeuralMind / Zeta Alpha, 2022-2023)

 corpus (BEIR dataset) --sample 100k docs--> GPT-J 6B, 3-shot MS MARCO "gbq" prompt,
                                             greedy, 1 query/doc   (~30 h on one A100)
        |
        v
 monoT5-3B (MS MARCO-tuned) scores all 100k (q, d) pairs  (~1.5 h on TPU v3-8)
        |  keep top 10k as positives
        v
 negatives: 1 random doc from BM25 top-1000 for each synthetic query
        |
        v
 fine-tune monoT5-3B (already MS MARCO-tuned) 1 epoch on 10k pos + 10k neg
 (Adafactor, lr 1e-3, batch 64+64; < 10 min on TPU v3-8)  -> one reranker per dataset
        |
 serve: BM25 top-1000 (Pyserini, k1=0.9, b=0.4) -> InPars-v2 monoT5-3B rerank

17.5.1 ANCE (Microsoft, 2020): global negatives from an asynchronously refreshed index

 Trainer (GPUs) --------- checkpoint k ---------> Inferencer (GPUs)
    ^                                              re-encode entire corpus
    |                                              rebuild ANN index (Faiss)
    +---- ANCE negatives = top-ranked docs <-------+  (swap in when done)
          from the (slightly stale) index

17.6 Distillation into retrievers and rerankers

  GPT-4 / R1-class LLM  --permutations / reasoning traces-->  7B-32B LLM reranker
          |                                                   (RankZephyr, Rank1)
          | permutation distillation (RankNet)                        |
          v                                                           v
  cross-encoder (DeBERTa / monoT5 / ModernBERT)  <--- scores --- (teacher for)
          |
          | MarginMSE / KL over top-k (GPL, ColBERTv2)
          v
  late-interaction (ColBERTv2)  /  bi-encoder (GPL student, E5)
          |
          | query-side-only or adapters to avoid re-indexing
          v
  production first stage

17.7 End-to-end domain adaptation playbook

 0. Baseline: BM25 + off-the-shelf embedder + off-the-shelf reranker; build an eval set
    of REAL user queries with graded labels (LLM-judged + human spot checks)
 1. Synthetic queries per chunk
      - generic LLM prompt  -> fast, MS-MARCO-like intents
      - few-shot task prompt (Promptagator) -> intent-specific
      - filter: reranker score (InPars-v2) or round-trip top-1 (Promptagator)
 2. Mine negatives
      - dense top-k with rank window + positive-aware margin (NV-Retriever)
      - denoise with cross-encoder (RocketQA) or keep soft scores (GPL)
 3. Train reranker first (cheapest big win; no re-index) -- BCE / listwise
      - optionally distil an LLM ranker's permutations (RankGPT) into it
 4. Train retriever
      - query-side only / adapter if re-embedding is expensive (Atlas, RA-DIT)
      - else full fine-tune with MarginMSE from the step-3 reranker (GPL)
      - optionally LM-supervised (LSR) if a target generator is fixed (REPLUG, RA-DIT)
      - merge with base weights to limit forgetting (Qwen3 / Gemini model soups)
 5. (G) If the generator misreads context: RAFT-style SFT with oracle + distractors,
      CoT with verbatim quotes, P < 100%; or RA-DIT LM-ft over top-k chunks
 6. (G) If facts must live in weights (offline / latency): synthetic CPT (EntiGraph),
      fact-level coverage, early stopping; keep RAG on top
 7. Re-evaluate end-to-end; track hallucination rate, not only retrieval metrics

18. Long-Term Memory Systems for LLMs and Agents: MemGPT/Letta, Mem0, Zep/Graphiti, ChatGPT and Claude Memory, LangMem, and the Write/Read Policies Behind Them

Overview

                     THE MEMORY LOOP (generic)
  +-----------+   turn    +-------------------+   response
  |   user /  |---------->|   LLM agent       |------------> user / env
  |   env     |           | (context window)  |
  +-----------+           +---------+---------+
                             ^      |  WRITE POLICY
                 READ POLICY |      |  (what / when / who / form)
                 (what to    |      v
                 inject,     |  +---------------------------+
                 when, how   |  | memory store(s)           |
                 much)       +--| profile | facts | graph | |
                                | episodes | files | logs   |
                                +---------------------------+
                                     ^  CONSOLIDATION / FORGETTING
                                     |  (dedupe, invalidate, summarize,
                                     +--  reflect, expire) - often offline

2.2 Architecture

  +--------------------------- MAIN CONTEXT (prompt tokens) --------------------------+
  | [system instructions] [working context: editable persona/user facts] [FIFO queue] |
  +-----------------------------------------------+-----------------------------------+
           ^  page in (search results)            |  page out (insert / evict)
           |                                      v
  +--------+---------------------+   +------------+-------------------------------+
  | RECALL STORAGE               |   | ARCHIVAL STORAGE                           |
  | full message log; search by  |   | read/write vector-backed DB of arbitrary   |
  | text / date                  |   | text; semantic search                      |
  +------------------------------+   +--------------------------------------------+
        QUEUE MANAGER: warns at ~70% of window ("memory pressure"); at 100% flushes
        ~half the queue and rewrites a recursive summary  |  FUNCTION EXECUTOR

3.2 Original architecture (2025 paper, v1/v2 library)

 new message pair (m_t-1, m_t)
        |
        v
 +------------------- EXTRACTION -------------------+
 | context = async-refreshed conversation summary S |
 |           + last m messages (default m=10)       |
 | LLM -> list of candidate facts                   |
 +-------------------------+------------------------+
                           v
 +-------------------- UPDATE ----------------------+
 | for each fact: vector-search top s similar       |
 | memories (default s=10)                          |
 | LLM tool call -> ADD | UPDATE | DELETE | NONE     |
 +-------------------------+------------------------+
                           v
             vector store (+ graph store for Mem0g)

4.2 Architecture

  EPISODES (raw messages / JSON / text, each with a reference timestamp)
     |  ingest: entity extraction (window of recent messages) -> entity resolution
     |          (embedding + full-text candidate match, LLM dedupe) -> fact (edge)
     |          extraction + dedupe -> temporal extraction -> contradiction check
     v
 +------------------------------------------------------------------------+
 |  Episode subgraph   : raw episodes; every derived fact links back here |
 |  Semantic subgraph  : entity nodes (evolving summaries) + fact edges   |
 |                       edge = (fact text, embedding, valid_at,          |
 |                       invalid_at, created_at, expired_at, episodes[])  |
 |  Community subgraph : clusters of entities with summaries              |
 +------------------------------------------------------------------------+
     |  retrieve: search (cosine | BM25 | breadth-first traversal)
     |            -> rerank (RRF | MMR | node distance | episode mentions |
     |               cross-encoder) -> context constructor (facts + dates)
     v
  prompt context string

5. LangMem and LangGraph Memory: A Toolkit View

          HOT PATH (conscious)                 BACKGROUND (subconscious)
  user -> agent --tool: manage_memory--> store <-- memory manager (after turn /
          agent --tool: search_memory--> store      after inactivity; reflects,
          (+latency, +tool-choice burden)           consolidates; no added latency)

6.2 Reverse-engineered architecture

  [system + developer instructions]
  [session metadata]        device/env info; injected per session, not persisted
  [user memory]             explicit long-term facts (saved memories)
  [recent conversations]    lightweight summaries of recent chats
  [current session window]  sliding window of this conversation's messages

19.11 Table QA without SQL: TableRAG and Chain-of-Table

TableRAG                                      Chain-of-Table
---------                                     --------------
question -> expand -> schema queries          table T0, question
                    -> cell queries             | plan op1 (e.g. f_select_row)
schema retrieval  (column embeddings)           v
cell retrieval    (distinct col=value pairs,  T1 -> plan op2 (f_group_by) -> T2
                   capped by encoding budget)   | ... until [E] (end)
        |                                       v
        v                                     final table -> LLM answer
compact prompt -> program-aided LM (e.g. Python/pandas) -> answer

20. Production RAG Operations: Ingestion, Freshness, Migrations, Latency, Cost, Observability, Caching and Debugging

1.1 The canonical ingestion pipeline

  sources (wiki, drive, tickets, code, DB)
        |  connectors: full crawl + webhooks / CDC / polling
        v
  +--------------+   +------------+   +-----------+   +------------------+
  | fetch + ACLs |-->| parse /    |-->| chunk +   |-->| change detection |
  | + metadata   |   | normalize  |   | enrich    |   | (hash per doc /  |
  +--------------+   +------------+   +-----------+   |  per chunk)      |
                                                      +--------+---------+
                                     unchanged: skip <---------+
                                                               | changed / new
                                                               v
                              +-------------------+    +----------------+
                              | embed (batch GPU  |--->| upsert vectors |
                              | or API, cached by |    | + lexical idx  |
                              | content hash)     |    | + metadata     |
                              +-------------------+    +-------+--------+
                                                               |
                       record manager / docstore  <------------+
                       (source_id -> {chunk ids, hashes, ts, model ver})
                                                               |
                                    cleanup: delete stale ids  v

4.2 Blue-green and named vectors (Qdrant pattern)

            writes ---------------+-------------------+
                                  v                   v
                         +--------------+     +--------------+
   queries --> alias --> | coll_v1      |     | coll_v2      | <-- backfill
               "docs"    | (model A)    |     | (model B)    |     worker
                         +--------------+     +--------------+
   cutover: point alias "docs" -> coll_v2 ; keep v1 for rollback window

5.1 Where the time goes

 client
   |-- auth/ACL lookup ............ 5-50 ms   (often underestimated)
   |-- query rewrite (LLM, opt.) .. 100-500 ms
   |-- query embedding ............ 5-50 ms
   |-- ANN + BM25 (parallel) ...... 5-100 ms  (cold object-store: 100s of ms)
   |-- rerank (cross-encoder) ..... 30-400 ms (depends on candidates x length)
   |-- prompt assembly + fetch .... 10-80 ms
   |-- LLM prefill  -> TTFT ....... dominant for long contexts
   '-- LLM decode ................. dominates total wall time

7.3 KV caching of retrieved knowledge

 retrieved: [c7][c2][c9]  ---> KV store (GPU/CPU/SSD) lookup per chunk
                                   |hit       |hit       |miss
                                   v          v          v
                              [KV c7]    [KV c2]   prefill c9
                                    \        |        /
                                selective recompute of ~few % tokens
                                             v
                                       decode answer

8.2 Standards: OpenTelemetry GenAI and OpenInference

 app (LangChain / LlamaIndex / custom)
   | OTel SDK + GenAI / OpenInference instrumentation
   v
 OTel collector --> trace store (Phoenix / Langfuse / APM vendor)
                      |
                      +--> sampled traces --> offline evaluators (faithfulness,
                      |                       context precision, citation check)
                      +--> dashboards: p95 per span, cache hit %, $/query,
                                       empty-retrieval %, index lag

9.2 Triage flow

 bad answer reported
   |
   v
 1. Replay trace. Is the correct source doc in the corpus?  --no--> FP1 / ingestion
   |yes                                                         (connector, parser,
   v                                                             filter, ACL?)
 2. Is the current version indexed? compare src hash vs index --stale--> freshness
   |yes                                                                 playbook
   v
 3. Was it in retriever top-N (pre-rerank)? --no--> FP2: query rewrite, hybrid,
   |yes                                              chunking, model version mix?
   v
 4. Survived rerank / context assembly?  --no--> FP3: reranker, top-k, token budget
   |yes
   v
 5. In prompt but answer wrong?  --yes--> FP4-FP7: prompt, model, format, citations
   |
   v
 6. Was a cache involved? semantic hit / stale prompt cache --> cache playbook

21. RAG System Design Interview Playbook: Enterprise Q&A, Support Bots, Code Assistants, and Web Answer Engines

1.3 The canonical component diagram

OFFLINE / ASYNC INGESTION PATH
 sources --> connectors/crawlers --> parse & clean --> chunk --> enrich
 (wiki, tickets,  (incremental,       (OCR, tables,    (struct-   (title, path,
  code, web)       webhooks, ACLs)     dedupe)          aware)     context blurb)
                                                                  |
          +-------------------+------------------+----------------+
          v                   v                  v
     lexical index       vector index       metadata / ACL store
     (BM25 / inverted)   (HNSW / IVF / BQ)  (doc->groups, freshness)

ONLINE ANSWER PATH
 user --> auth --> query understanding --> hybrid retrieve --> fuse (RRF)
          |        (rewrite, condense,     (BM25 + dense,     |
          |         route, decompose)       ACL pre-filter)   v
          |                                              rerank (cross-enc.)
          |                                                   |
          +--> conversation memory          context assembly (top-k, order,
                                            dedupe, token budget)
                                                   |
                                                   v
                         LLM generation with citations --> guardrails --> user
                                                   |
                        logs, feedback, LLM-judge <-+--> eval sets, dashboards

2.4 Traffic and tokens

avg_QPS   = DAU x queries_per_user_per_day / 86,400
peak_QPS  ~ 3-10 x avg_QPS (state assumption)
input_tok = system_prompt + history + k_chunks x chunk_tokens + question
LLM_cost  = (input_tok x p_in + output_tok x p_out) x queries

2.5 Latency budget (interactive chat, illustrative)

auth + ACL lookup           10-30 ms
query rewrite (small LLM)   100-300 ms   (skip when query is self-contained)
hybrid retrieval            20-100 ms    (BM25 + ANN in parallel)
rerank top-50..100          50-200 ms    (cross-encoder, GPU)
LLM time-to-first-token     300-1000 ms  (depends on model and prompt size)
guardrail check             50-500 ms    (cheap similarity first, LLM judge if needed)

3.3 Architecture

 +-----------+   webhooks / polling   +-------------------+
 | SaaS apps |----------------------->| connector workers |--+
 | (wiki,    |   (per-source rate     | fetch content +   |  |
 |  drive,   |    limits, backoff)    | ACLs + activity   |  |
 |  tickets) |                        +-------------------+  |
 +-----------+                                               v
                       +----------------------------------------------+
                       | normalize -> chunk -> embed -> index         |
                       | per-chunk fields: tenant, acl_principals[],  |
                       | source, updated_at, author, doc_type         |
                       +---------------------+------------------------+
                                             |
         +---------------+-------------------+--------------+
         v               v                                  v
   inverted index   vector index (tenant-sharded)   knowledge graph
   (BM25)           with ACL filter fields          (people, docs, teams,
                                                     activity edges)
 ONLINE:
 user -> SSO -> expand principals (user + groups, cached) -> query rewrite
      -> parallel BM25 + ANN with filter acl_principals IN user_principals
      -> RRF -> rerank (+authority/recency/graph features) -> top 5-10
      -> LLM answer with inline citations -> post-check: cited doc still visible?

4.4 Architecture

 customer msg --> session store (history, account ctx, locale)
       |
       v
 intent + condense (small LLM): "core issue" + entities (order id, product)
       |
       +--> policy router: sensitive intent? (fraud, legal, safety) --> human
       |
       v
 retrieval: help-center (hybrid) + resolved-ticket KG / similar cases
       |                                  + tool calls (order status API)
       v

       |
       v

       |pass                                 |fail or timeout
       v                                     v
   send to user                       escalate to agent with summary
       |
       v
 offline: LLM-judge sampling, CSAT, resolution, repeat-contact, reopen rate

5.4 Architecture

 IDE plugin                                   server
 +-----------------------------+              +-------------------------------+
 | cursor ctx (prefix/suffix)  |  completion  | low-latency code LLM (FIM)    |
 | open-tab snippet matcher    |------------->| prompt cache                  |
 | local symbol index (LSP)    |  <~ hundreds |                               |
 | Merkle tree of workspace    |  of ms total +-------------------------------+
 +-------------+---------------+
               | diff sync (changed hashes only)
               v
 +---------------------------------------------------------------+
 | chunk by syntax (functions/classes) -> embed (async, cached   |
 | by content hash) -> per-repo/branch vector index + code search |
 | + symbol/code graph                                           |
 +-----------------------------+---------------------------------+
                               |  chat / agent queries
                               v
 retrievers: keyword(+rewrite) | embeddings | graph (defs/refs) | open files
         -> rank (recsys-style) -> pack under token budget -> chat LLM
         -> answer/edit; agent may iterate (grep, read file, run tests)

6.3 Reference architecture: Perplexity on Vespa

  crawler fleet --> fetch queue (priority by freshness/authority)
       |                       ^
       v                       | recrawl signals (news, change rate)
  parse + boilerplate removal + spam/quality classifiers
       |
  split into sections/spans --> per-span: text, embedding, signals
       |
       v
  distributed index (sharded by doc id), lexical + vector + attributes
 ----------------------------------------------------------------------
 ONLINE (~hundreds of ms budget for retrieval):
 query --> classify (navigational / fresh / complex) --> rewrite / decompose
       --> fan-out to all shards: hybrid retrieval of spans
       --> stage-1 cheap rank (per shard) --> merge top-N
       --> stage-2 ML ranker (many signals) --> stage-3 cross-encoder (top-K)
       --> LLM synthesize answer with numbered citations (streamed)
       --> complex queries: iterate (search -> read -> search) as agent
